# T2.5 filesystem 安全操作实现计划

> **面向 AI 代理的工作者：** 必需子技能：使用
> `superpowers:subagent-driven-development`（推荐）或
> `superpowers:executing-plans` 逐任务实现此计划。步骤使用复选框（`- [ ]`）
> 语法跟踪进度；每个任务提交前必须先做独立规格审查，再做独立代码质量审查。

**目标：** 实现 Windows 上失败关闭的 `internal/filesystem`：以固定祖先和 leaf
句柄完成路径授权、Runtime 日志、state、download staging、受控递归删除及原子
rename，确保检查到副作用之间没有按路径重新打开的替换窗口。

**架构：** `path_windows.go` 统一规范路径、查询物理身份并建立不授予
`FILE_SHARE_DELETE` 的祖先 pin；三类专用能力分别把 Runtime 日志 token、state
snapshot 和 download session 绑定到 owner、volume serial、128-bit file ID 与同一
打开句柄。通用 `Operator` 只接受封闭的 `DeleteKind` / `RenameKind`，重新用
`config.Layout` 绑定精确身份，递归删除和 rename 均以 handle-relative Win32 API
执行；副作用成功后先冻结 `MutationApplied` / `Published` / `Removed`，再报告关闭
错误。State 原子写以 `StateWriteError` 明确报告 create / write / sync / rename /
close 阶段和独立 cleanup 错误，不要求消费方解析诊断字符串。

**技术栈：** Go 1.26，标准库 `context`、`crypto/sha256`、`errors`、`io`、
`path/filepath`、`sync`、`time`、`testing`；固定依赖
`golang.org/x/sys/windows v0.42.0`；Windows 10/11、PowerShell 7、Win32
`CreateFileW` / `GetFileInformationByHandleEx` /
`SetFileInformationByHandle` / `DuplicateHandle` / `FlushFileBuffers`。

**设计依据：** [T2.5 filesystem 安全操作设计](./设计-T2.5-filesystem安全操作.md)、
[架构设计“文件与删除安全边界”](./架构设计.md#文件与删除安全边界)、
[任务拆分 T2.5](./任务拆分.md)。冻结设计提交为 `626b060`；实现基线必须在任务 0
执行时通过 `git rev-parse HEAD` 动态记录，不在计划中固化会过期的代码基线。

---

## 文件结构

- 保留 `internal/filesystem/doc.go`
  - 现有中文包注释已经准确，不修改。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/path_windows.go`
  - 定义公共错误类型、`CanonicalPath`、路径规范化、volume / file ID / link count /
    case-sensitive 查询、固定祖先句柄和 handle-relative Win32 基础操作。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/path_windows_test.go`
  - 白盒测试规范路径、错误链、句柄连续性、UNC / remote /
    per-directory case-sensitive 拒绝和底层故障注入。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/runtime_logs.go`
  - 定义 Runtime 日志能力、writer、不可伪造文件 token、`RemoveResult` 和公共方法。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/runtime_logs_windows.go`
  - 实现日志目录创建与固定、append writer、直接文件列举和 handle disposition 删除。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/runtime_logs_windows_test.go`
  - 测试祖先 pin、硬链接、token 身份、父能力 close 与 writer 独立生命周期。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/state_files.go`
  - 定义 `StateFileKind`、snapshot、原子写阶段、类型化错误、结果和公共方法。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/state_files_windows.go`
  - 实现有界读取、同目录临时文件、replace rename、同句柄条件删除和 RWMutex close。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/state_files_windows_test.go`
  - 测试 size limit、读写 share mode、snapshot 身份、故障阶段及 barrier 并发。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/download_files.go`
  - 定义 download 能力、session、publish / abort 结果和不可逆状态机。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/download_files_windows.go`
  - 实现 `.part` create-exclusive、同句柄写入、no-replace 发布和失败清理。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/download_files_windows_test.go`
  - 测试 stale part、同名竞争、发布 / abort 状态、file ID 连续性和关闭故障。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/delete.go`
  - 先定义删除枚举、请求、结果、审计接口与审计错误；Task 9 再同时引入
    `Operator`、`Option`、私有 `options` 和构造器。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/delete_windows.go`
  - 实现 Layout 身份授权、逐项递归删除、双阶段审计和独立收口 context。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/delete_test.go`
  - 测试封闭枚举、字段校验、允许列表、保护名单和审计状态机。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/delete_windows_test.go`
  - 测试固定句柄递归、Reparse Point、部分删除、取消和删除故障注入。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/rename.go`
  - 先定义 rename 枚举、请求、结果和等待器；Task 9 再与 `options` 同时引入有限
    重试 Option。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/rename_windows.go`
  - 实现精确源目标授权、固定源与目标父句柄、no-replace rename 和错误映射。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/rename_windows_test.go`
  - 测试三种 rename、延迟序列、context、占用、竞争目标和 post-rename close 故障。
- 修改 `go.mod`
  - 首个实现任务固定 `golang.org/x/sys v0.42.0`；后续任务只验证，不重复引入或降级。
- 创建 `go.sum`
  - 记录固定依赖校验和。
- 修改 `doc/任务拆分.md`
  - 全部门禁与审查通过后，单独回写 T2.5 完成日期和代码提交短哈希。

首版只提供 `_windows.go` 实现，不添加非 Windows 伪实现。`internal/filesystem`
只能依赖 `internal/config`、`internal/protocol` 和 `golang.org/x/sys/windows`；
不得依赖 `cli`、`gitrepo`、`logging`、`state` 或 `uv`。

## 全局实现约束

1. 所有路径只能来自传入的 `config.Layout` getter；`internal/filesystem` 不硬编码
   `repo`、`runtime-state`、`logs/runtime`、缓存或 state 文件名。
2. 规范化只产生值对象。任何写、删或 rename 必须从 app-root 开始逐级持有不授予
   `FILE_SHARE_DELETE` 的祖先 handle，并在同一个已核验 leaf handle 上完成
   disposition / rename；禁止回退到 `os.RemoveAll`、`os.Remove`、`os.Rename` 或
   路径版 `MoveFileExW`。
3. 每个已存在组件以
   `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT | FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS` 打开。任何
   Reparse Point、无法查询 file ID / volume / attributes / case sensitivity 的组件都
   失败关闭。
4. 危险能力只支持本地 volume。UNC 可以由 `Canonicalize` 解析用于诊断，但
   `Operator`、`RuntimeLogFiles`、`StateFiles`、`DownloadFiles` 拒绝 UNC、
   `DRIVE_REMOTE` 和任一祖先启用 `FILE_CS_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE_DIR` 的布局。
5. 所有接收 `ctx` 的 API 在任何 I/O 前拒绝 nil；逐级打开、遍历、等待和每个新副作用
   前检查取消。nil 必须包装 `ErrInvalidArgument`，调用前已经取消必须满足
   `errors.Is(err, context.Canceled)`，且两者 Win32 / Auditor 调用计数均为 0。
   提交点之后观察到的取消不能覆盖已经冻结的 applied / published / removed 结果。
   生产和测试禁止 `time.Sleep`。
6. `RuntimeLogFiles` 用 mutex 线性化 open/list/remove/close；writer 独立复制三层
   祖先 pin。`StateFiles` 用 RWMutex 使 Close 等待已开始的 Read。`DownloadFiles`
   构造器只同步验证 Layout 和当前已存在祖先，返回前关闭全部校验 handle；对象只保存
   不可变 Layout 与测试依赖。每次 `Begin` 都重新逐级创建、打开并固定
   app-root/runtime/cache/downloads 四层祖先，把四层 pin 和 part leaf 一并交给
   新 session；不得永久持有 base pin、不得增加公共 `Close` 或 finalizer。
7. `WriteAtomicResult.MutationApplied`、`RenameResult.MutationApplied`、
   `RemoveResult.MutationApplied`、`PublishResult.Published` 和
   `AbortResult.Removed` 必须在 Win32 副作用成功后、关闭 handle 前冻结。关闭失败不能
   把已生效结果改回 false，也不能自动回滚或再次 Abort。`WriteAtomic` 一旦开始 I/O，
   create-temp、write、flush、发布前 identity / destination 检查、rename 和发布后
   close 失败必须分别返回冻结阶段的 `StateWriteError`；主错误只进 `Cause`，清理
   disposition / close 错误只进 `CleanupError`，二者由 `Unwrap() []error` 同时保链。
8. 删除 `started` 审计失败时零副作用；started 成功后必须用
   `context.WithTimeout(context.WithoutCancel(operationCtx), 5*time.Second)`
   尝试 finished。删除错误、取消错误和 finished 审计错误使用 `errors.Join` 保留
   每条错误链。
9. 测试故障注入只通过同包私有 constructor / function-field dependency 实现，不增加
   公共 Option。每个 public constructor 创建自己的不可变 production `pathAPI`，
   禁止包级可变替身；测试只调用下节冻结的 `...With` 私有入口。测试替身必须响应 ctx。
10. 新增注释全部为中文并以被注释标识符开头；error 字符串使用英文、小写开头、无句号。
    包不写 stdout，不发 protocol 事件。

## 冻结私有依赖与 Windows 打开矩阵

为避免故障注入变成包级竞态，以下私有签名也在计划内冻结。生产 wrapper 每次构造
局部、不可变的 `pathAPI`；测试把 function fields 放入值参数，不修改包级变量。

```go
type objectIdentity struct {
	volumeSerial  uint64
	fileID        [16]byte
	attributes    uint32
	numberOfLinks uint32
	size          int64
}

type directoryEntry struct {
	name       string
	attributes uint32
}

type openSpec struct {
	access    uint32
	share     uint32
	creation  uint32
	options   uint32
	directory bool
}

type pathAPI struct {
	makeDirectory   func(path string) error
	attributes      func(path string) (uint32, error)
	openPath        func(path string, spec openSpec) (windows.Handle, error)
	openRelative    func(parent windows.Handle, name string, spec openSpec) (windows.Handle, error)
	finalPath       func(handle windows.Handle) (string, error)
	identity        func(handle windows.Handle) (objectIdentity, error)
	caseSensitive   func(handle windows.Handle) (bool, error)
	driveType       func(root string) (uint32, error)
	duplicateHandle func(handle windows.Handle) (windows.Handle, error)
	listDirectory   func(handle windows.Handle) ([]directoryEntry, error)
	readFile        func(handle windows.Handle, buffer []byte) (int, error)
	writeFile       func(handle windows.Handle, buffer []byte) (int, error)
	flushFile       func(handle windows.Handle) error
	setDisposition  func(handle windows.Handle) error
	rename          func(
		source windows.Handle,
		targetParent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		replace bool,
	) error
	closeHandle func(handle windows.Handle) error
}

func newProductionPathAPI() pathAPI
func (a pathAPI) valid() bool

type pinnedObject struct {
	path     CanonicalPath
	handle   windows.Handle
	identity objectIdentity
}

type pinnedChain struct {
	api     pathAPI
	objects []pinnedObject
}

func openPinnedChain(
	ctx context.Context,
	rootParent CanonicalPath,
	target CanonicalPath,
	leafSpec openSpec,
) (*pinnedChain, error)

func openPinnedChainWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	rootParent CanonicalPath,
	target CanonicalPath,
	leafSpec openSpec,
	api pathAPI,
) (*pinnedChain, error)

func validateDangerousRoot(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	targets []string,
) ([]*pinnedChain, error)

func validateDangerousRootWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	targets []string,
	api pathAPI,
) ([]*pinnedChain, error)

func openRelativeCheckedWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	parent pinnedObject,
	name string,
	spec openSpec,
	api pathAPI,
) (pinnedObject, error)

func validateParentIdentityWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	parent pinnedObject,
	child pinnedObject,
	api pathAPI,
) error

func deleteByHandleWith(handle windows.Handle, api pathAPI) error
func renameByHandleWith(
	source windows.Handle,
	targetParent windows.Handle,
	name string,
	replace bool,
	api pathAPI,
) error
func (c *pinnedChain) close() error
```

`validateParentIdentityWith` 从 child 的最终 handle path 取得物理父路径，在 fixed parent
仍持有且不授予 share-delete 时以 open-reparse-point 打开物理父目录，比较 volume
serial 和 128-bit file ID 后立即关闭验证 handle；不接受字符串相等替代 file ID。
`pinnedChain.close` 必须使用 `c.api.closeHandle` 逆序关闭，确保注入的 close 故障与打开
该 chain 的同一个 API 值配对。

owner marker 必须是非零大小的私有类型，避免不同零大小对象的指针被 Go 实现合并：

```go
type runtimeLogOwner struct {
	marker byte
}

type stateOwner struct {
	marker byte
}
```

四类能力和派生对象固定按以下方式保存、复制同一个 `pathAPI` 值；任何方法都不得临时改用
`newProductionPathAPI()`，否则测试无法覆盖 write / close / disposition / rename 故障：

```go
type RuntimeLogFiles struct {
	mu sync.Mutex // 保护 closed、closeErr、pins 以及 open/list/remove/close 的线性化。

	layout   *config.Layout
	api      pathAPI
	owner    *runtimeLogOwner
	pins     [3]pinnedObject
	closed   bool
	closeErr error
}

type RuntimeLogWriter struct {
	mu sync.Mutex // 保护 file、pins、closed 和 closeErr。

	api      pathAPI
	path     string
	file     pinnedObject
	pins     [3]pinnedObject
	closed   bool
	closeErr error
}

type RuntimeLogFile struct {
	owner        *runtimeLogOwner
	path         string
	name         string
	volumeSerial uint64
	fileID       [16]byte
}

type StateFiles struct {
	mu sync.RWMutex // 保护 closed、closeErr、pins，并线性化 read/write/remove/close。

	layout   *config.Layout
	api      pathAPI
	owner    *stateOwner
	pins     [2]pinnedObject
	closed   bool
	closeErr error
}

type StateFileSnapshot struct {
	owner        *stateOwner
	kind         StateFileKind
	volumeSerial uint64
	fileID       [16]byte
	digest       [sha256.Size]byte
	payload      []byte
}

type DownloadFiles struct {
	layout *config.Layout
	api    pathAPI
}

type DownloadSession struct {
	mu sync.Mutex // 保护 part、pins、state、结果和 closeErr。

	api       pathAPI
	path      string
	partPath  string
	part      pinnedObject
	pins      [4]pinnedObject
	state     downloadState
	published PublishResult
	abort     AbortResult
	closeErr  error
}
```

`NewRuntimeLogFiles`、`NewStateFiles`、`NewDownloadFiles` 和 `New` 仅在公共入口创建一次
`newProductionPathAPI()`；private constructor 校验 `api.valid()` 后把该值存入返回对象。
`OpenAppend` 把 `f.api` 复制到 writer，`Begin` 把 `f.api` 复制到 session，
`openPinnedChainWith` 把实参复制到 chain，`New` 把 dependencies.api 存入 Operator；
派生方法和清理路径只能继续使用已保存值。测试从完整 production API 值复制一份后仅替换
目标字段，不允许 nil 未使用字段掩盖依赖遗漏。

Download session 没有公共 Close。故障注入测试需要强制释放仍打开 handle 时，在
`download_files_windows_test.go`（同包测试）精确定义：

```go
func (s *DownloadSession) closeForTest() error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	closeErrors := make([]error, 0, len(s.pins)+1)
	closeOne := func(handle *windows.Handle) {
		if *handle == 0 || *handle == windows.InvalidHandle {
			return
		}
		if err := s.api.closeHandle(*handle); err != nil {
			closeErrors = append(closeErrors, err)
			return
		}
		*handle = windows.InvalidHandle
	}
	closeOne(&s.part.handle)
	for i := len(s.pins) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		closeOne(&s.pins[i].handle)
	}
	return errors.Join(closeErrors...)
}
```

该 helper 持有 `s.mu`，只用 `s.api.closeHandle` 关闭仍打开的 part 与四层 pins，
不 disposition、不 rename、不改变公开状态机；只允许由 `t.Cleanup` 调用。生产文件、
消费包和正常绿灯路径不得引用它。`DownloadFiles` 本身没有 handle，因此不得定义
`(*DownloadFiles).closeForTest`。

各能力 private constructor 固定为：

```go
type runtimeLogDependencies struct {
	api pathAPI
}

func newRuntimeLogFilesWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	dependencies runtimeLogDependencies,
) (*RuntimeLogFiles, error)

type stateFileDependencies struct {
	api pathAPI
}

func newStateFilesWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	dependencies stateFileDependencies,
) (*StateFiles, error)

type downloadFileDependencies struct {
	api pathAPI
}

func newDownloadFilesWith(
	layout *config.Layout,
	dependencies downloadFileDependencies,
) (*DownloadFiles, error)

type operatorDependencies struct {
	api             pathAPI
	finishedContext func(context.Context) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc)
}

func newWithDependencies(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	auditor Auditor,
	opts options,
	dependencies operatorDependencies,
) (*Operator, error)
```

`Operator` 在 Task 9 定义时必须包含 `layout *config.Layout`、`auditor Auditor`、
`api pathAPI`、`wait WaitFunc`、防御性复制的 `delays []time.Duration` 和
`finishedContext`；构造验证使用的临时 chains 在返回前全部关闭，操作时再用 `o.api`
重新固定路径。

所有 `CreateFileW` / relative open 都必须从下表选择完整 `openSpec`，并在测试中逐字段
断言 access、share、creation、options 和 directory；任何能力不得自行拼另一组权限。

| 能力 | access | share | creation | options | directory |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Canonicalize 已存在前缀 | `FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | 按已存在前缀实际类型 |
| 目录 pin / list root / rename target parent | `FILE_LIST_DIRECTORY \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `true` |
| parent-identity 验证 handle | `FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `true` |
| Runtime log append leaf | `FILE_APPEND_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_ALWAYS` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Runtime log List leaf | `FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Runtime log Remove leaf | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State Read leaf | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State atomic temp leaf | `FILE_WRITE_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| DELETE \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ` | `CREATE_NEW` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State 正式 destination 安全检查 | `FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State conditional Remove leaf | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| DELETE \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Download final inspection | `FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Download stale part removal | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Download new part | `FILE_WRITE_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| DELETE \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ` | `CREATE_NEW` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| 递归删除 file | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| 递归删除 directory | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| FILE_LIST_DIRECTORY \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `true` |
| Rename source directory | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `true` |

Canonicalize 的前缀 open 是只读快照，允许 share-delete；任何危险能力随后仍须重新从
app-root 固定祖先，不能复用已经关闭的规范化 handle。parent-identity 行只用于打开 child
最终物理路径的父目录，并与仍固定的 parent 比较 file ID；不得复用 Canonicalize 行。
State destination 检查 handle 保持到 rename 返回，并要求 ordinary、非 Reparse、
`NumberOfLinks == 1` 和 parent identity 一致；不存在是唯一可跳过该行的分支。
Runtime log append leaf 的 share 必须精确等于
`windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE`，明确不含
`windows.FILE_SHARE_DELETE`；这既允许不同 `RuntimeLogFiles` 实例同时 append，又使
任一活动 writer 都能阻止 leaf 被删除、重命名或替换。

每个 leaf 打开成功后必须调用带 ctx 的
`validateParentIdentityWith(ctx, parent, child, api)`，失败时通过同一个
`api.closeHandle` 关闭 leaf 且零副作用。Task 1～11 的权限测试合起来必须实际触发上表
每一行，并逐字段比较五列，不能只断言 access 或 share。

带 context 的 14 个公共 API 与零 I/O 测试固定映射如下；每个测试均表驱动覆盖 nil 和
调用前已取消两行，不能只覆盖其中一种：

| 公共 API | 固定测试 |
| --- | --- |
| `New` | `TestNew_ContextRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `NewRuntimeLogFiles` / `OpenAppend` | `TestRuntimeLogFiles_ContextRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `List` / `Remove` | `TestRuntimeLogFiles_ListAndRemoveContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `NewStateFiles` / `Read` | `TestStateFiles_ReadContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `WriteAtomic` / `RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged` | `TestStateFiles_WriteAndRemoveContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `Begin` | `TestDownloadFiles_BeginContextRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `PublishNoReplace` / `Abort` | `TestDownloadSession_PublishAndAbortContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `RemoveTree` | `TestRemoveTree_ContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `AtomicRename` | `TestAtomicRename_ContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |

## 公共 PowerShell 7 前置与 helper

每个任务开始时在
`D:\Github\AUTO-MAS-Runtime\.worktrees\codex-runtime-implementation`
使用 `pwsh` 执行：

```powershell
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$go = (Get-Command go -ErrorAction Stop).Source
$gofmt = (Get-Command gofmt -ErrorAction Stop).Source
$rg = (Get-Command rg -ErrorAction Stop).Source
$env:GOCACHE = Join-Path $env:TEMP "auto-mas-runtime-t25"

function Invoke-Checked {
    param(
        [scriptblock]$Command,
        [string]$Name
    )

    $output = @(& $Command 2>&1)
    $exitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    $output
    if ($exitCode -ne 0) {
        throw "$Name failed with exit code $exitCode"
    }
}

function Invoke-ExpectedFailure {
    param(
        [string]$Pattern,
        [string]$ExpectedFailure
    )

    $output = @(& $go test ./internal/filesystem -run $Pattern -count=1 -v 2>&1)
    $exitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    $output
    $text = $output -join [Environment]::NewLine
    if ($exitCode -eq 0) {
        throw "red test unexpectedly passed"
    }
    if ($text -match "\[no tests to run\]" -or
        $text -notmatch "(build failed|--- FAIL:)") {
        throw "red test did not execute and fail"
    }
    if ($text -notmatch $ExpectedFailure) {
        throw "red test failed for an unexpected reason"
    }
}

function Invoke-PassingTest {
    param(
        [string]$Pattern,
        [int]$Count = 1
    )

    $output = @(& $go test ./internal/filesystem -run $Pattern -count=$Count -v 2>&1)
    $exitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    $output
    $text = $output -join [Environment]::NewLine
    if ($exitCode -ne 0) {
        throw "targeted test failed with exit code $exitCode"
    }
    if ($text -match "\[no tests to run\]" -or $text -notmatch "--- PASS:") {
        throw "targeted test did not produce a passing result"
    }
}

function Assert-XSysPin {
    param([string]$Task)

    $xsysVersion = (& $go list -m -f '{{.Version}}' "golang.org/x/sys").Trim()
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task x/sys version lookup failed" }
    if ($xsysVersion -ne "v0.42.0") {
        throw "$Task x/sys version = $xsysVersion, want v0.42.0"
    }

    $requirements = @(
        Select-String `
            -LiteralPath "go.mod" `
            -Pattern '^\s*golang\.org/x/sys\s+v0\.42\.0(?:\s+// indirect)?\s*$'
    )
    if ($requirements.Count -ne 1) {
        throw "$Task go.mod must contain exactly one x/sys v0.42.0 requirement"
    }
    $wrongVersions = @(
        Select-String `
            -LiteralPath "go.mod" `
            -Pattern 'golang\.org/x/sys\s+v(?!0\.42\.0(?:\s|$))\S+'
    )
    if ($wrongVersions.Count -ne 0) {
        $wrongVersions
        throw "$Task found a conflicting x/sys version"
    }
}

function Invoke-StandardGate {
    param([string]$Task)

    Assert-XSysPin -Task $Task

    $unformatted = @(& $gofmt -l .)
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task gofmt check failed" }
    if ($unformatted.Count -ne 0) {
        $unformatted
        throw "$Task gofmt found unformatted files"
    }

    & $go vet ./...
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task go vet failed" }
    & $go build -buildvcs=false ./...
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task go build failed" }
    & $go test ./... -count=1
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task go test failed" }
    & git diff --check
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task git diff check failed" }
}

function Submit-Task {
    param(
        [string]$Task,
        [string]$Message,
        [string[]]$Files
    )

    Invoke-StandardGate -Task $Task

    $before = @(
        & git -c core.quotePath=false diff --cached --name-only
    )
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task pre-stage check failed" }
    if ($before.Count -ne 0) {
        $before
        throw "$Task found pre-existing staged files"
    }

    $stagedByInvocation = @()
    try {
        & git add -- $Files
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task staging failed" }

        $staged = @(
            & git -c core.quotePath=false diff --cached --name-only
        )
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task staged list failed" }
        $stagedByInvocation = @(
            $staged | Where-Object { $_ -notin $before }
        )
        $delta = @(
            Compare-Object `
                ($stagedByInvocation | Sort-Object) `
                ($Files | Sort-Object)
        )
        if ($delta.Count -ne 0) {
            $delta
            throw "$Task staged unexpected files"
        }

        & git diff --cached --check
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task staged diff check failed" }
        & git commit -m $Message
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task commit failed" }
    } catch {
        if ($stagedByInvocation.Count -eq 0) {
            $current = @(
                & git -c core.quotePath=false diff --cached --name-only
            )
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
                $stagedByInvocation = @(
                    $current |
                        Where-Object {
                            $_ -notin $before -and $_ -in $Files
                        }
                )
            }
        }
        if ($stagedByInvocation.Count -ne 0) {
            & git restore --staged -- $stagedByInvocation
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
                throw "$Task failed and could not restore its staged files"
            }
        }
        throw
    }
}
```

所有原生命令都必须立即读取并检查 `$LASTEXITCODE`。定向测试必须同时证明退出码为 0、
输出不含 `[no tests to run]` 且出现 `--- PASS:`。红灯必须验证预期 API 缺失或指定
断言失败，不能接受工具链、权限或测试正则错误。`Submit-Task` 在任何 staging / commit
之前强制执行完整 gofmt / vet / build / test / diff-check 标准门，因此 Task 1～13 的
每个非空提交都必须以当次全绿证据为前提。两个 staged-file 快照都关闭
`core.quotePath`，中文路径按原样比较；helper 拒绝已有暂存内容，失败时只
`git restore --staged` 本次调用新增的文件，不修改用户原有暂存、工作树内容或提交历史。

## 任务 0：实现前依赖、设计、计划与干净基线硬门

**文件：**

- 不修改任何文件。

本任务只验证前置条件。任一检查失败都立即停止，不删除或移动现场，不用
`git clean`、`git reset`、`git stash` 或 `git checkout` 代替用户裁定。

- [ ] **步骤 1：确认 worktree、分支、T2.1 与冻结设计**

```powershell
$expectedRoot = (
    Resolve-Path -LiteralPath `
        "D:\Github\AUTO-MAS-Runtime\.worktrees\codex-runtime-implementation" `
        -ErrorAction Stop
).Path
$actualRootText = (& git rev-parse --show-toplevel).Trim()
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 worktree lookup failed" }
$actualRoot = (
    Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $actualRootText -ErrorAction Stop
).Path
if (-not [StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase.Equals(
    $actualRoot,
    $expectedRoot
)) {
    throw "T2.5 unexpected worktree: $actualRoot"
}

$branch = (& git branch --show-current).Trim()
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 branch lookup failed" }
if ($branch -ne "codex/runtime-implementation") {
    throw "T2.5 unexpected branch: $branch"
}

$t21CompletionCommit = "ab927a1"
& git merge-base --is-ancestor $t21CompletionCommit HEAD
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    throw "T2.1 completion commit is not an ancestor of HEAD"
}
$t21Progress = @(
    & $rg -n --fixed-strings -- `
        '- [x] **T2.1 config 与目录布局**（M）' `
        "doc/任务拆分.md"
)
$t21ProgressExit = $LASTEXITCODE
$t21Progress
if ($t21ProgressExit -gt 1) { throw "T2.1 progress lookup failed" }
if ($t21ProgressExit -eq 1 -or $t21Progress.Count -ne 1) {
    throw "T2.1 must satisfy DoD and be recorded complete before T2.5"
}

& $go test ./internal/config -count=100
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.1 config tests failed" }

$designCommit = "626b060"
& git cat-file -e "$designCommit`^{commit}"
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 design commit is missing" }
& git merge-base --is-ancestor $designCommit HEAD
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    throw "T2.5 design commit must be current or an ancestor of HEAD"
}
```

预期：Git 返回的 `/` 路径与固定 worktree 的 `\` 路径都先经 `Resolve-Path`
规范为绝对路径，再按 `OrdinalIgnoreCase` 精确匹配；分支精确匹配，T2.1 的实现提交和进度记录均已进入当前历史，
`internal/config` 重复测试通过，`626b060` 是当前提交或其祖先。T2.1 进度尚未回写时，
先执行已经批准的 T2.1 收口计划并由用户处理提交；不得在 T2.5 实现提交中顺带修改。

- [ ] **步骤 2：确认本计划已经获批并提交**

```powershell
$planPath = "doc/计划-T2.5-filesystem安全操作.md"
$trackedPlan = @(& git ls-files --error-unmatch -- $planPath)
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or $trackedPlan.Count -ne 1) {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan must be tracked and committed"
}

& git diff --quiet -- $planPath
$planWorktreeExit = $LASTEXITCODE
if ($planWorktreeExit -gt 1) { throw "T2.5 plan worktree check failed" }
if ($planWorktreeExit -eq 1) {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan has uncommitted worktree changes"
}

& git diff --cached --quiet -- $planPath
$planIndexExit = $LASTEXITCODE
if ($planIndexExit -gt 1) { throw "T2.5 plan index check failed" }
if ($planIndexExit -eq 1) {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan has uncommitted staged changes"
}

$planCommit = (& git log -1 --format=%H -- $planPath).Trim()
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 plan commit lookup failed" }
if ($planCommit -notmatch '^[0-9a-f]{40}$') {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan has no valid commit"
}
& git merge-base --is-ancestor $planCommit HEAD
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan commit is not in current history"
}
```

脚本只能证明计划已跟踪、无未提交变化且提交位于当前历史；开始执行前还必须已有用户对
该提交内容的明确批准。批准前不得把“计划已写完”解释为实现授权。

- [ ] **步骤 3：显式处理 ignored `.superpowers/` 与全部 untracked 计划**

```powershell
& git check-ignore --quiet --no-index -- ".superpowers/"
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    throw ".superpowers/ must remain ignored"
}

$untrackedPlans = @(
    & git -c core.quotePath=false ls-files `
        --others `
        --exclude-standard `
        -- "doc/计划-*.md"
)
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "untracked plan lookup failed" }
if ($untrackedPlans.Count -ne 0) {
    $untrackedPlans
    throw "untracked plans must be committed or resolved by the user"
}

$statusLines = @(
    & git -c core.quotePath=false status `
        --porcelain=v1 `
        --untracked-files=all
)
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 clean-worktree check failed" }
if ($statusLines.Count -ne 0) {
    $statusLines
    throw "T2.5 implementation requires a fully clean worktree and index"
}
```

`.superpowers/` 可以存在，但必须继续被 `.gitignore` 排除；本计划绝不删除它。完整
status 检查在任何 pathspec 过滤前执行。当前 T2.2、T2.3、T2.4、T2.5 等 untracked
计划必须由用户决定提交、移动到仓库外或保留并暂停 T2.5，执行者不得自行删除、暂存或
藏匿。

- [ ] **步骤 4：运行未引入 x/sys 前的完整基线门并动态记录 HEAD**

```powershell
$unformatted = @(& $gofmt -l .)
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline gofmt check failed" }
if ($unformatted.Count -ne 0) {
    $unformatted
    throw "T2.5 baseline contains unformatted Go files"
}
& $go vet ./...
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline go vet failed" }
& $go build -buildvcs=false ./...
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline build failed" }
& $go test ./... -count=1
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline tests failed" }
& git diff --check
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline diff check failed" }

$script:T25BaselineCommit = (& git rev-parse --verify HEAD).Trim()
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline commit lookup failed" }
if ($script:T25BaselineCommit -notmatch '^[0-9a-f]{40}$') {
    throw "T2.5 baseline commit is invalid"
}
& git merge-base --is-ancestor $designCommit $script:T25BaselineCommit
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    throw "T2.5 baseline must contain the frozen design commit"
}
"T2.5 implementation baseline: $script:T25BaselineCommit"
```

后续任务在同一 `pwsh` 会话执行；若会话重启，必须从任务 0 重新运行。动态值用于最终
审查实现提交范围；它只在已验证包含 `626b060` 后记录，不写入版本库，也不得替换为
设计文档中过期的历史基线。

## 任务 1：公共错误、封闭枚举与 Windows 规范路径

**文件：**

- [ ] **步骤 1：先引入并核验唯一固定依赖**

测试文件从第一行开始就导入 `golang.org/x/sys/windows`，因此必须在编译红灯前建立
模块解析环境；该动作不实现任何 filesystem API：

```powershell
& $go get "golang.org/x/sys@v0.42.0"
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 x/sys install failed" }
Assert-XSysPin -Task "T2.5 Task 1 dependency"
```

检查 `go.mod` / `go.sum` 只引入 `v0.42.0` 所需记录。T2.4 若已先引入该版本，本步骤
必须幂等复用；这是整份计划唯一允许执行 `go get golang.org/x/sys...` 的位置。Task 2
以后只调用 `Assert-XSysPin`，不得再次 `go get`、`go mod edit` 或降级依赖。

- [ ] **步骤 2：编写固定名称的失败测试**

先创建 `path_windows_test.go`。以下代码块是该文件的完整首版内容，不是函数名清单；
原样落盘后必须能被 Go 编译器解析并进入目标红灯：

```go
package filesystem

import (
	"errors"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"unsafe"

	"golang.org/x/sys/windows"

	"github.com/AUTO-MAS-Project/AUTO-MAS-Runtime/internal/protocol"
)

func TestCanonicalize_RejectsUnsafeSyntaxBeforeIO(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	tests := []struct {
		name string
		path string
	}{
		{name: "empty", path: ""},
		{name: "nul", path: "C:\\AUTO-MAS\x00\\repo"},
		{name: "drive relative", path: `C:repo`},
		{name: "dot", path: `.`},
		{name: "parent relative", path: `..\repo`},
		{name: "physical device", path: `\\.\PhysicalDrive0`},
		{name: "global root", path: `\\?\GLOBALROOT\Device`},
		{name: "reserved tail", path: `C:\AUTO-MAS\CON`},
		{name: "alternate stream", path: `C:\AUTO-MAS\name:stream`},
		{name: "empty component", path: `C:\AUTO-MAS\\repo`},
		{name: "trailing dot", path: `C:\AUTO-MAS\repo.`},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			t.Parallel()

			api := newProductionPathAPI()
			api.attributes = func(string) (uint32, error) {
				t.Fatal("attributes called for invalid syntax")
				return 0, nil
			}
			api.openPath = func(string, openSpec) (windows.Handle, error) {
				t.Fatal("openPath called for invalid syntax")
				return windows.InvalidHandle, nil
			}
			got, err := canonicalizeWith(test.path, api)
			if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument) {
				t.Fatalf("canonicalizeWith(%q) error = %v, want ErrInvalidArgument", test.path, err)
			}
			if got != (CanonicalPath{}) {
				t.Fatalf("canonicalizeWith(%q) = %#v, want zero value", test.path, got)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCanonicalize_NormalizesEquivalentForms(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	mixed := filepath.Join(root, "MiXeD")
	if err := os.Mkdir(mixed, 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.Mkdir() error = %v", err)
	}

	canonical := mustCanonicalize(t, mixed)
	forms := []string{
		mixed + `\`,
		filepath.Join(root, "mixed"),
		extendedPathForTest(mixed),
	}
	for _, form := range forms {
		got := mustCanonicalize(t, form)
		if !canonical.Equal(got) {
			t.Errorf("Canonicalize(%q).Equal(canonical) = false, want true", form)
		}
	}
	if !filepath.IsAbs(canonical.String()) {
		t.Fatalf("String() = %q, want absolute path", canonical.String())
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(canonical.Native(), `\\?\`) {
		t.Fatalf("Native() = %q, want extended prefix", canonical.Native())
	}
}

func TestCanonicalize_ExpandsExistingShortPath(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	longName := filepath.Join(root, "Directory With A Long Name")
	if err := os.Mkdir(longName, 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.Mkdir() error = %v", err)
	}
	shortName, ok := shortPathForTest(t, longName)
	if !ok {
		t.Skip("volume does not expose an 8.3 short path for the fixture")
	}

	longCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, longName)
	shortCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, shortName)
	if !longCanonical.Equal(shortCanonical) {
		t.Fatalf("long.Equal(short) = false, want true")
	}
}

func TestCanonicalize_AllowsOnlySafeNonexistentTail(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	target := filepath.Join(root, "future", "leaf")
	got := mustCanonicalize(t, target)
	parent := mustCanonicalize(t, root)
	if !parent.Contains(got) {
		t.Fatalf("Canonicalize(root).Contains(target) = false, want true")
	}
	if got.String() != target {
		t.Fatalf("String() = %q, want %q", got.String(), target)
	}
}

func TestCanonicalPath_ContainsUsesVolumeAndComponentBoundary(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	parent := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS`)
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		child CanonicalPath
		want  bool
	}{
		{name: "same path", child: canonicalPathForTest(`c:\auto-mas`), want: false},
		{name: "direct child", child: canonicalPathForTest(`c:\auto-mas\repo`), want: true},
		{name: "deep child", child: canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\repo\src`), want: true},
		{name: "sibling prefix", child: canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS-2`), want: false},
		{name: "component prefix", child: canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS-old\repo`), want: false},
		{name: "other volume", child: canonicalPathForTest(`D:\AUTO-MAS\repo`), want: false},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			t.Parallel()
			if got := parent.Contains(test.child); got != test.want {
				t.Fatalf("Contains(%q) = %t, want %t", test.child.String(), got, test.want)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCanonicalPath_UsesOrdinalNonASCIIEquivalence(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	upper := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\Äpfel`)
	lower := canonicalPathForTest(`c:\auto-mas\äPFEL`)
	if !upper.Equal(lower) {
		t.Fatal("ordinal case variants are not equal")
	}
	parent := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\Äpfel`)
	child := canonicalPathForTest(`c:\auto-mas\äPFEL\cache`)
	if !parent.Contains(child) {
		t.Fatal("ordinal case variant child is not contained")
	}

	composed := canonicalPathForTest("C:\\AUTO-MAS\\\u00e9")
	decomposed := canonicalPathForTest("C:\\AUTO-MAS\\e\u0301")
	if composed.Equal(decomposed) {
		t.Fatal("composed and decomposed forms are ordinal-equal, want distinct")
	}
}

func TestCanonicalPath_OrdinalComparisonFailureFailsClosed(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	valid := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\repo`)
	badVolume := valid
	badVolume.volumeKey = "C:\x00"
	if badVolume.Equal(valid) || badVolume.Contains(valid) {
		t.Fatal("invalid volume comparison authorized a path")
	}

	badPath := valid
	badPath.comparisonKey = "C:\\AUTO-MAS\x00\\repo"
	descendant := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\repo\child`)
	if badPath.Equal(valid) || badPath.Contains(descendant) {
		t.Fatal("invalid full-path comparison authorized a path")
	}
}

func TestCanonicalize_ExistingPrefixMatchesAccessMatrix(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	file := filepath.Join(root, "state.json")
	if err := os.WriteFile(file, []byte("{}"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
	}

	tests := []struct {
		name      string
		path      string
		directory bool
	}{
		{name: "directory", path: root, directory: true},
		{name: "file", path: file, directory: false},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			api := newProductionPathAPI()
			openPath := api.openPath
			closeHandle := api.closeHandle
			var got openSpec
			closeCalls := 0
			api.openPath = func(path string, spec openSpec) (windows.Handle, error) {
				got = spec
				return openPath(path, spec)
			}
			api.closeHandle = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
				closeCalls++
				return closeHandle(handle)
			}

			if _, err := canonicalizeWith(test.path, api); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("canonicalizeWith() error = %v", err)
			}
			want := openSpec{
				access:    windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES | windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
				share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE | windows.FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
				creation:  windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
				options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS | windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
				directory: test.directory,
			}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
				t.Fatalf("openSpec = %#v, want %#v", got, want)
			}
			if closeCalls != 1 {
				t.Fatalf("closeHandle calls = %d, want 1", closeCalls)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestFilesystemEnums_StringAndValid(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	type enum interface {
		String() string
		Valid() bool
	}
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		value enum
		want  string
	}{
		{name: "state backend", value: StateBackend, want: "backend"},
		{name: "state mutation", value: StateMutation, want: "mutation"},
		{name: "state update", value: StateUpdate, want: "update"},
		{name: "state environment", value: StateEnvironment, want: "environment"},
		{name: "state write create", value: StateWritePhaseCreate, want: "create"},
		{name: "state write write", value: StateWritePhaseWrite, want: "write"},
		{name: "state write sync", value: StateWritePhaseSync, want: "sync"},
		{name: "state write rename", value: StateWritePhaseRename, want: "rename"},
		{name: "state write close", value: StateWritePhaseClose, want: "close"},
		{name: "delete uv cache", value: DeleteUVCache, want: "uv_cache"},
		{name: "delete managed venv", value: DeleteManagedVenv, want: "managed_venv"},
		{name: "delete managed python", value: DeleteManagedPython, want: "managed_python"},
		{name: "delete repo update", value: DeleteRepositoryUpdate, want: "repository_update"},
		{name: "delete repo retired", value: DeleteRepositoryRetired, want: "repository_retired"},
		{name: "delete temporary", value: DeleteDownloadTemporary, want: "download_temporary"},
		{name: "delete uv staging", value: DeleteUVStaging, want: "uv_staging"},
		{name: "delete pycache", value: DeletePythonCache, want: "python_cache"},
		{name: "delete build cache", value: DeleteBuildCache, want: "build_cache"},
		{name: "audit started", value: DeleteAuditStarted, want: "started"},
		{name: "audit finished", value: DeleteAuditFinished, want: "finished"},
		{name: "rename retired", value: RenameRepositoryToRetired, want: "repository_to_retired"},
		{name: "rename repository", value: RenameUpdateToRepository, want: "update_to_repository"},
		{name: "rename uv", value: RenameUVStagingToVersion, want: "uv_staging_to_version"},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			if !test.value.Valid() {
				t.Fatal("Valid() = false, want true")
			}
			if got := test.value.String(); got != test.want {
				t.Fatalf("String() = %q, want %q", got, test.want)
			}
		})
	}

	invalid := []enum{
		StateFileKind("unknown"),
		StateWritePhase("unknown"),
		DeleteKind("unknown"),
		DeleteAuditPhase("unknown"),
		RenameKind("unknown"),
	}
	for _, value := range invalid {
		if value.Valid() {
			t.Errorf("%T(%q).Valid() = true, want false", value, value.String())
		}
	}
}

func TestFilesystemErrors_PreserveChainsAndCodes(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	cause := errors.New("injected")
	pathErr := &Error{
		code:      protocol.CodePathOutsideManagedRoot,
		Operation: "authorize",
		Path:      `C:\AUTO-MAS\repo`,
		Err:       cause,
	}
	if !errors.Is(pathErr, cause) {
		t.Fatalf("errors.Is(%v, cause) = false, want true", pathErr)
	}
	if got := pathErr.Code(); got != protocol.CodePathOutsideManagedRoot {
		t.Fatalf("Code() = %q, want %q", got, protocol.CodePathOutsideManagedRoot)
	}

	fileErr := &FileError{Operation: "open", Path: pathErr.Path, Err: cause}
	if !errors.Is(fileErr, cause) {
		t.Fatalf("errors.Is(%v, cause) = false, want true", fileErr)
	}
	tooLarge := &FileError{
		Operation: "size-limit",
		Path:      pathErr.Path,
		Err:       ErrStateFileTooLarge,
	}
	if !errors.Is(tooLarge, ErrStateFileTooLarge) {
		t.Fatalf("errors.Is(%v, ErrStateFileTooLarge) = false, want true", tooLarge)
	}
	auditErr := &AuditError{
		Phase:           DeleteAuditFinished,
		MutationApplied: true,
		Cause:           cause,
	}
	if !errors.Is(auditErr, cause) {
		t.Fatalf("errors.Is(%v, cause) = false, want true", auditErr)
	}

	cleanup := errors.New("cleanup failed")
	writeErr := &StateWriteError{
		Phase:           StateWritePhaseRename,
		MutationApplied: false,
		Cause:           fileErr,
		CleanupError:    cleanup,
	}
	if !errors.Is(writeErr, cause) || !errors.Is(writeErr, cleanup) {
		t.Fatalf("StateWriteError causes = %v, want primary and cleanup", writeErr)
	}
	if writeErr.Phase != StateWritePhaseRename || writeErr.MutationApplied {
		t.Fatalf("StateWriteError = %#v, want rename/not-applied", writeErr)
	}
}

func TestWindows_CanonicalPathLongAndShortFormsCannotBypassProtection(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	longPath := root
	for i := 0; i < 12; i++ {
		longPath = filepath.Join(longPath, strings.Repeat("segment", 4))
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(extendedPathForTest(longPath), 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.MkdirAll() error = %v", err)
	}
	longCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, longPath)
	extendedCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, extendedPathForTest(longPath))
	if !longCanonical.Equal(extendedCanonical) {
		t.Fatal("long and extended forms are not equal")
	}

	sibling := mustCanonicalize(t, longPath+"-other")
	if longCanonical.Contains(sibling) {
		t.Fatal("sibling prefix was accepted as a descendant")
	}
	if shortPath, ok := shortPathForTest(t, longPath); ok {
		shortCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, shortPath)
		if !longCanonical.Equal(shortCanonical) {
			t.Fatal("long and 8.3 forms are not equal")
		}
	}
}

func mustCanonicalize(t *testing.T, path string) CanonicalPath {
	t.Helper()
	got, err := Canonicalize(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Canonicalize(%q) error = %v", path, err)
	}
	return got
}

func canonicalPathForTest(path string) CanonicalPath {
	clean := filepath.Clean(path)
	volume := filepath.VolumeName(clean)
	return CanonicalPath{
		display:       clean,
		native:        extendedPathForTest(clean),
		comparisonKey: clean,
		volumeKey:     volume,
	}
}

func extendedPathForTest(path string) string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(path, `\\?\`) {
		return path
	}
	if strings.HasPrefix(path, `\\`) {
		return `\\?\UNC\` + strings.TrimPrefix(path, `\\`)
	}
	return `\\?\` + path
}

func shortPathForTest(t *testing.T, path string) (string, bool) {
	t.Helper()

	pathUTF16, err := windows.UTF16PtrFromString(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("windows.UTF16PtrFromString() error = %v", err)
	}
	buffer := make([]uint16, 32768)
	procedure := windows.NewLazySystemDLL("kernel32.dll").NewProc("GetShortPathNameW")
	length, _, callErr := procedure.Call(
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pathUTF16)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&buffer[0])),
		uintptr(len(buffer)),
	)
	if length == 0 {
		if errors.Is(callErr, windows.ERROR_SUCCESS) {
			t.Fatalf("GetShortPathNameW(%q) returned no path", path)
		}
		return "", false
	}
	if length >= uintptr(len(buffer)) {
		t.Fatalf("GetShortPathNameW length = %d, buffer = %d", length, len(buffer))
	}
	short := windows.UTF16ToString(buffer[:length])
	if short == path || !strings.Contains(short, "~") {
		return "", false
	}
	return short, true
}
```

测试 helper 只用精确字符串相等和 `~` 标记判断 volume 是否实际提供了不同的 8.3
别名，不把大小写比较用于授权。测试对身份的断言全部通过 `Equal` / `Contains`。

`TestCanonicalize_RejectsUnsafeSyntaxBeforeIO` 表驱动传空字符串、NUL、`C:repo`、
`.`、`..\repo`、`\\.\PhysicalDrive0`、`\\?\GLOBALROOT\Device`、不存在尾部中的
`CON`、`name:stream`、空组件和尾部点，断言 `errors.Is(err,
ErrInvalidArgument)`。测试注入一个一旦调用就 `t.Fatal` 的 path probe，证明纯语法错误
在任何 Win32 查询前拒绝。

等价路径表只断言公开 `Equal` / `Contains` 结果，固定覆盖普通绝对路径、尾部反斜杠、
大小写变体、`\\?\` 形式、可用时的 8.3 短路径和超过 `MAX_PATH` 的普通组件链；
严禁断言 `comparisonKey` / `volumeKey` 的原始字节相等。`Native()` 必须以 `\\?\`
或 `\\?\UNC\` 开头，`String()` 必须是绝对显示路径。`Contains` 表明确断言根本身为
false、直接后代和多级后代为 true、`AUTO-MAS-2` 与组件 `repo-old` 为 false、其他
volume 为 false。

非 ASCII 表固定断言 `Äpfel` / `äPFEL` 在同一组件位置 ordinal ignore-case 等价，
预组合 `é` 与分解序列 `e\u0301` 不等价；测试不得调用 `strings.ToLower`、
`strings.EqualFold`、Unicode normalization 或任何 locale-sensitive 比较。
`TestCanonicalPath_OrdinalComparisonFailureFailsClosed` 用同包私有字段构造含 NUL 的
volume 与完整路径各一例，使 `windows.UTF16FromString` 返回错误，逐项断言 `Equal` /
`Contains` 都为 false 且不 panic；这证明任一 ordinal 调用失败都会关闭授权。已存在
前缀测试捕获 `openSpec`，逐字段断言
Canonicalize 行的 access/share/creation/options；目录前缀断言 `directory=true`，
最终已存在普通文件断言 `directory=false`，并证明规范化结束即关闭该只读 handle。

枚举测试必须逐值列出四个 `StateFileKind`、五个 `StateWritePhase`、九个
`DeleteKind`、两个 `DeleteAuditPhase` 和三个 `RenameKind`，对合法值断言
`String()` 等于冻结字面量且 `Valid()` 为 true，对零值和 `"unknown"` 断言 false。

Windows 对抗测试在真实 `t.TempDir()` 下建立超过 `MAX_PATH` 的组件链；若 volume
启用 8.3，则用 `GetShortPathNameW` 取得同一目录短名。长名、短名、大小写变体和
`\\?\` 形式必须 Equal，且任何形式都不能把 sibling 前缀误判为受保护目录后代。

```powershell
Invoke-ExpectedFailure `

    "(undefined: Canonicalize|undefined: ErrInvalidArgument|undefined: StateWritePhaseCreate|undefined: DeleteUVCache)"
```

- [ ] **步骤 4：建立公共类型**

本任务只把“冻结公共契约”中的公共值类型、请求、结果、接口、枚举和 sentinel
分别放入对应主题文件；除 `Canonicalize`、值对象 getter、枚举 `String` / `Valid`
和错误方法外，不提前定义公共方法。构造器与 I/O 方法在各自对应的 `_windows.go`
任务中一次定义，避免先提交会被误用的半实现。**本任务不定义 `Operator`、
`Option`、私有 `options`、`New`、`WithWait` 或 `WithRenameDelays`**；这些声明与实现
在 Task 9 同一提交引入。公共错误实现固定使用：

```go
func (e *Error) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s %q: %v", e.Operation, e.Path, e.Err)
}

func (e *Error) Unwrap() error       { return e.Err }
func (e *Error) Code() protocol.Code { return e.code }

func (e *FileError) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s %q: %v", e.Operation, e.Path, e.Err)
}

func (e *FileError) Unwrap() error { return e.Err }

type operationCleanupError struct {
	cause   error
	cleanup error
}

func (e *operationCleanupError) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%v; cleanup: %v", e.cause, e.cleanup)
}

func (e *operationCleanupError) Unwrap() []error {
	if e == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return []error{e.cause, e.cleanup}
}

func joinOperationCleanup(cause, cleanup error) error {
	if cause == nil {
		return cleanup
	}
	if cleanup == nil {
		return cause
	}
	if combined, ok := cause.(*operationCleanupError); ok {
		return &operationCleanupError{
			cause:   combined.cause,
			cleanup: errors.Join(combined.cleanup, cleanup),
		}
	}
	return &operationCleanupError{cause: cause, cleanup: cleanup}
}

func splitOperationCleanup(err error) (error, error) {
	if combined, ok := err.(*operationCleanupError); ok {
		return combined.cause, combined.cleanup
	}
	return err, nil
}

func (e *AuditError) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("record %s deletion audit: %v", e.Phase, e.Cause)
}

func (e *AuditError) Unwrap() error { return e.Cause }

func (e *StateWriteError) Error() string {
	if e == nil {
		return "state write failed"
	}
	if e.Cause == nil {
		return fmt.Sprintf("state write %s failed", e.Phase)
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("state write %s: %v", e.Phase, e.Cause)
}

func (e *StateWriteError) Unwrap() []error {
	if e == nil {
		return nil
	}
	causes := make([]error, 0, 2)
	if e.Cause != nil {
		causes = append(causes, e.Cause)
	}
	if e.CleanupError != nil {
		causes = append(causes, e.CleanupError)
	}
	return causes
}
```

所有 `String` 对未知值返回原始字符串；`Valid` 使用 switch，不保存可变 map。红灯输出
必须匹配步骤 3 的目标 API 缺失；若出现 `no required module provides package`、
`missing go.sum entry` 或网络/代理错误，说明步骤 1 未完成，不能把该结果算作 TDD 红灯。

五组枚举方法不得只保留声明，完整加入：

```go
func (k StateFileKind) String() string {
	return string(k)
}

func (k StateFileKind) Valid() bool {
	switch k {
	case StateBackend, StateMutation, StateUpdate, StateEnvironment:
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}
}

func (p StateWritePhase) String() string {
	return string(p)
}

func (p StateWritePhase) Valid() bool {
	switch p {
	case StateWritePhaseCreate,
		StateWritePhaseWrite,
		StateWritePhaseSync,
		StateWritePhaseRename,
		StateWritePhaseClose:
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}
}

func (k DeleteKind) String() string {
	return string(k)
}

func (k DeleteKind) Valid() bool {
	switch k {
	case DeleteUVCache,
		DeleteManagedVenv,
		DeleteManagedPython,
		DeleteRepositoryUpdate,
		DeleteRepositoryRetired,
		DeleteDownloadTemporary,
		DeleteUVStaging,
		DeletePythonCache,
		DeleteBuildCache:
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}
}

func (p DeleteAuditPhase) String() string {
	return string(p)
}

func (p DeleteAuditPhase) Valid() bool {
	switch p {
	case DeleteAuditStarted, DeleteAuditFinished:
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}

func (k RenameKind) String() string {
	return string(k)
}

func (k RenameKind) Valid() bool {
	switch k {
	case RenameRepositoryToRetired,
		RenameUpdateToRepository,
		RenameUVStagingToVersion:
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 5：实现 Canonicalize 的最小完整算法**

`path_windows.go` 一次实现“冻结私有依赖与 Windows 打开矩阵”中的
`objectIdentity`、`directoryEntry`、`openSpec`、`pathAPI`、`pinnedObject` 和
`pinnedChain`，并增加：

```go
func canonicalizeWith(path string, api pathAPI) (CanonicalPath, error)
func compareStringOrdinal(left, right string, ignoreCase bool) (int32, error)
func canonicalComponents(path string) []string
```

实现顺序固定为：

1. 用 `filepath.VolumeName` 和显式设备前缀检查拒绝空、相对、volume-relative、NUL 和
   device namespace；不读取 cwd。
2. 对绝对路径词法清理；从完整路径向父级回退，找到第一个存在的前缀。
3. 按矩阵的 Canonicalize 行以 open-reparse-point 打开已存在前缀，通过
   `GetFinalPathNameByHandleW` 展开真实长名称，但不解析 Reparse Point 目标；拼回已经
   逐组件验证的不存在尾部，并在返回值前关闭前缀 handle。
4. 从“Windows API 已展开的存在前缀 + 已验证的不存在尾部”生成 `display`，再生成
   `\\?\` / `\\?\UNC\` 形式的 `native`；超过 `MAX_PATH` 仍不得截断。
5. `comparisonKey` 只保存上述已经规范化、分隔符已统一、非根尾斜杠已移除的结构
   规范串；`volumeKey` 保存同样规范化的 drive / UNC share spelling。二者都**不是**
   `CompareStringOrdinal` 生成的 fold key，不得用 `==`、map key 或裸字符串前缀作授权。
6. 设计要求的 Windows ordinal、大小写无关等价关系由 `Equal` / `Contains` 在比较时
   直接调用 `CompareStringOrdinal(..., TRUE)` 实现。`Contains` 先 ordinal 比较
   volume，再把两个结构规范串切成完整组件，要求 child 组件更多且每个 parent 组件
   ordinal 等价；不得按 UTF-8 字节长度截取前缀。

Windows 没有提供“返回 CompareStringOrdinal 折叠后字符串”的 API；上述做法只调整私有
表示，不改变设计要求的可观测等价关系。大小写、8.3、长路径和非 ASCII 的等价/不等价
全部由固定测试证明，不能自行换成 Unicode lowercase 或 normalization。

`compareStringOrdinal` 的生产签名和核心实现固定如下；它不暴露可变包级 syscall
替身，Windows 调用失败时 `Equal` / `Contains` 返回 false，保证授权失败关闭：

```go
const cstrEqual int32 = 2

func compareStringOrdinal(left, right string, ignoreCase bool) (int32, error) {
	leftUTF16, err := windows.UTF16FromString(left)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("encode left ordinal string: %w", err)
	}
	rightUTF16, err := windows.UTF16FromString(right)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("encode right ordinal string: %w", err)
	}

	ignoreCaseValue := uintptr(0)
	if ignoreCase {
		ignoreCaseValue = 1
	}
	procedure := windows.NewLazySystemDLL("kernel32.dll").
		NewProc("CompareStringOrdinal")
	result, _, callErr := procedure.Call(
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&leftUTF16[0])),
		uintptr(int32(len(leftUTF16)-1)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&rightUTF16[0])),
		uintptr(int32(len(rightUTF16)-1)),
		ignoreCaseValue,
	)
	if result == 0 {
		if errors.Is(callErr, windows.ERROR_SUCCESS) {
			callErr = errors.New("compare string ordinal returned no result")
		}
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("compare string ordinal: %w", callErr)
	}
	return int32(result), nil
}

func canonicalComponents(path string) []string {
	volume := filepath.VolumeName(path)
	remainder := strings.Trim(strings.TrimPrefix(path, volume), `\`)
	if remainder == "" {
		return nil
	}
	return strings.Split(remainder, `\`)
}

func (p CanonicalPath) Equal(other CanonicalPath) bool {
	if p.comparisonKey == "" || other.comparisonKey == "" {
		return false
	}
	volumeResult, err := compareStringOrdinal(
		p.volumeKey,
		other.volumeKey,
		true,
	)
	if err != nil || volumeResult != cstrEqual {
		return false
	}
	pathResult, err := compareStringOrdinal(
		p.comparisonKey,
		other.comparisonKey,
		true,
	)
	return err == nil && pathResult == cstrEqual
}

func (p CanonicalPath) Contains(other CanonicalPath) bool {
	if p.comparisonKey == "" || other.comparisonKey == "" {
		return false
	}
	volumeResult, err := compareStringOrdinal(
		p.volumeKey,
		other.volumeKey,
		true,
	)
	if err != nil || volumeResult != cstrEqual {
		return false
	}
	parent := canonicalComponents(p.comparisonKey)
	child := canonicalComponents(other.comparisonKey)
	if len(child) <= len(parent) {
		return false
	}
	for i := range parent {
		result, err := compareStringOrdinal(parent[i], child[i], true)
		if err != nil || result != cstrEqual {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}
```

`newProductionPathAPI` 填满每个 function field；完整性检查固定为：

```go
func (a pathAPI) valid() bool {
	return a.makeDirectory != nil &&
		a.attributes != nil &&
		a.openPath != nil &&
		a.openRelative != nil &&
		a.finalPath != nil &&
		a.identity != nil &&
		a.caseSensitive != nil &&
		a.driveType != nil &&
		a.duplicateHandle != nil &&
		a.listDirectory != nil &&
		a.readFile != nil &&
		a.writeFile != nil &&
		a.flushFile != nil &&
		a.setDisposition != nil &&
		a.rename != nil &&
		a.closeHandle != nil
}
```

`Canonicalize` 只创建局部 production API 并调用 `canonicalizeWith`，测试仅把完整 API
值作为参数注入，不得增加公共 Option 或可重绑定包级变量。

`Canonicalize`、输入清理、存在前缀展开和结构串生成固定采用以下完整最小实现。
`api.attributes` 与 `api.openPath` 都必须使用扩展长度路径，且 close
失败不能返回看似有效的值对象：

```go
func Canonicalize(path string) (CanonicalPath, error) {
	return canonicalizeWith(path, newProductionPathAPI())
}

func canonicalizeWith(path string, api pathAPI) (CanonicalPath, error) {
	if !api.valid() {
		return CanonicalPath{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: incomplete path api", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	cleaned, err := cleanAbsoluteWindowsPath(path)
	if err != nil {
		return CanonicalPath{}, err
	}

	existing := cleaned
	tail := make([]string, 0, 4)
	var attributes uint32
	for {
		attributes, err = api.attributes(nativeWindowsPath(existing))
		if err == nil {
			break
		}
		if !isWindowsNotFound(err) {
			return CanonicalPath{}, &FileError{
				Operation: "attributes",
				Path:      existing,
				Err:       err,
			}
		}
		volume := filepath.VolumeName(existing)
		if isWindowsVolumeRoot(existing, volume) {
			return CanonicalPath{}, &FileError{
				Operation: "attributes",
				Path:      existing,
				Err:       err,
			}
		}
		name := filepath.Base(existing)
		if err := validateNonexistentComponent(name); err != nil {
			return CanonicalPath{}, err
		}
		tail = append(tail, name)
		parent := filepath.Dir(existing)
		if parent == existing {
			return CanonicalPath{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: path has no existing prefix", ErrInvalidArgument)
		}
		existing = parent
	}

	spec := openSpec{
		access:    windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES | windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE | windows.FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
		creation:  windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
		options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS | windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
		directory: attributes&windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY != 0,
	}
	handle, err := api.openPath(nativeWindowsPath(existing), spec)
	if err != nil {
		return CanonicalPath{}, &FileError{
			Operation: "open-canonical-prefix",
			Path:      existing,
			Err:       err,
		}
	}
	finalPath, finalErr := api.finalPath(handle)
	closeErr := api.closeHandle(handle)
	if finalErr != nil || closeErr != nil {
		return CanonicalPath{}, joinOperationCleanup(
			wrapFileError("final-path", existing, finalErr),
			wrapFileError("close", existing, closeErr),
		)
	}

	display, err := displayWindowsPath(finalPath)
	if err != nil {
		return CanonicalPath{}, err
	}
	for i := len(tail) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		display = filepath.Join(display, tail[i])
	}
	comparisonKey := structuralWindowsPath(display)
	volume := filepath.VolumeName(comparisonKey)
	if volume == "" {
		return CanonicalPath{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: path has no volume", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	volumeKey := structuralWindowsPath(volume)
	return CanonicalPath{
		display:       display,
		native:        nativeWindowsPath(display),
		comparisonKey: comparisonKey,
		volumeKey:     volumeKey,
	}, nil
}

func cleanAbsoluteWindowsPath(path string) (string, error) {
	if path == "" || strings.ContainsRune(path, '\x00') {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: path is empty or contains nul", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	normalized, err := displayWindowsPath(strings.ReplaceAll(path, "/", `\`))
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	upper := strings.ToUpper(normalized)
	if strings.HasPrefix(upper, `\\.\`) ||
		strings.HasPrefix(upper, `\??\`) ||
		strings.HasPrefix(upper, `\\?\GLOBALROOT\`) ||
		strings.HasPrefix(upper, `\\?\VOLUME{`) {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: device path is not allowed", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	volume := filepath.VolumeName(normalized)
	if volume == "" || !filepath.IsAbs(normalized) {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: path is not absolute", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if hasInteriorEmptyComponent(normalized, volume) {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: path contains an empty component", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	return filepath.Clean(normalized), nil
}

func displayWindowsPath(path string) (string, error) {
	switch {
	case strings.HasPrefix(path, `\\?\UNC\`):
		return `\\` + strings.TrimPrefix(path, `\\?\UNC\`), nil
	case strings.HasPrefix(path, `\\?\`):
		withoutPrefix := strings.TrimPrefix(path, `\\?\`)
		if len(filepath.VolumeName(withoutPrefix)) != 2 {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: unsupported extended path", ErrInvalidArgument)
		}
		return withoutPrefix, nil
	case strings.HasPrefix(path, `\\.\`):
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: device path is not allowed", ErrInvalidArgument)
	default:
		return path, nil
	}
}

func nativeWindowsPath(path string) string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(path, `\\?\`) {
		return path
	}
	if strings.HasPrefix(path, `\\`) {
		return `\\?\UNC\` + strings.TrimPrefix(path, `\\`)
	}
	return `\\?\` + path
}

func structuralWindowsPath(path string) string {
	cleaned := filepath.Clean(strings.ReplaceAll(path, "/", `\`))
	volume := filepath.VolumeName(cleaned)
	remainder := strings.Trim(strings.TrimPrefix(cleaned, volume), `\`)
	if remainder == "" {
		return strings.TrimRight(volume, `\`) + `\`
	}
	return strings.TrimRight(cleaned, `\`)
}

func hasInteriorEmptyComponent(path, volume string) bool {
	remainder := strings.TrimPrefix(path, volume)
	remainder = strings.TrimPrefix(remainder, `\`)
	remainder = strings.TrimSuffix(remainder, `\`)
	return strings.Contains(remainder, `\\`)
}

func validateNonexistentComponent(name string) error {
	if name == "" || name == "." || name == ".." ||
		strings.HasSuffix(name, ".") || strings.HasSuffix(name, " ") ||
		strings.ContainsAny(name, `<>:"/\|?*`) || isReservedWindowsName(name) {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: unsafe nonexistent path component %q", ErrInvalidArgument, name)
	}
	return nil
}

func isReservedWindowsName(name string) bool {
	base := strings.ToUpper(strings.SplitN(name, ".", 2)[0])
	switch base {
	case "CON", "PRN", "AUX", "NUL", "CONIN$", "CONOUT$":
		return true
	}
	if len(base) != 4 || base[3] < '1' || base[3] > '9' {
		return false
	}
	return base[:3] == "COM" || base[:3] == "LPT"
}

func isWindowsNotFound(err error) bool {
	return errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND) ||
		errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_PATH_NOT_FOUND)
}

func isWindowsVolumeRoot(path, volume string) bool {
	if volume == "" {
		return false
	}
	result, err := compareStringOrdinal(
		structuralWindowsPath(path),
		structuralWindowsPath(volume+`\`),
		true,
	)
	return err == nil && result == cstrEqual
}

func wrapFileError(operation, path string, err error) error {
	if err == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return &FileError{Operation: operation, Path: path, Err: err}
}

func (p CanonicalPath) String() string {
	return p.display
}

func (p CanonicalPath) Native() string {
	return p.native
}
```

上面 `strings.ToUpper` 只用于识别固定 ASCII device namespace 和保留设备名，不用于
路径身份；`isWindowsVolumeRoot` 仍通过 ordinal 比较。不存在尾部的组件按原始顺序验证，
已存在前缀由 handle 的最终长路径展开，因此 8.3、扩展前缀和大小写变体最终都只能通过
`Equal` / `Contains` 判定身份。

`newProductionPathAPI` 不能只列字段名或留空 wrapper。Task 1 同时加入以下完整绑定与
Win32 adapter；后续任务只能调用保存的 `pathAPI` 值，不再直接调用对应 syscall：

```go
func newProductionPathAPI() pathAPI {
	return pathAPI{
		makeDirectory:   makeDirectoryWindows,
		attributes:      attributesWindows,
		openPath:        openPathWindows,
		openRelative:    openRelativeWindows,
		finalPath:       finalPathWindows,
		identity:        identityWindows,
		caseSensitive:   caseSensitiveWindows,
		driveType:       driveTypeWindows,
		duplicateHandle: duplicateHandleWindows,
		listDirectory:   listDirectoryWindows,
		readFile:        readFileWindows,
		writeFile:       writeFileWindows,
		flushFile:       windows.FlushFileBuffers,
		setDisposition:  setDispositionWindows,
		rename:          renameWindows,
		closeHandle:     windows.CloseHandle,
	}
}

func makeDirectoryWindows(path string) error {
	pathUTF16, err := windows.UTF16PtrFromString(path)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("encode directory path: %w", err)
	}
	if err := windows.CreateDirectory(pathUTF16, nil); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("create directory: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func attributesWindows(path string) (uint32, error) {
	pathUTF16, err := windows.UTF16PtrFromString(path)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("encode attribute path: %w", err)
	}
	attributes, err := windows.GetFileAttributes(pathUTF16)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("get file attributes: %w", err)
	}
	return attributes, nil
}

func openPathWindows(path string, spec openSpec) (windows.Handle, error) {
	pathUTF16, err := windows.UTF16PtrFromString(path)
	if err != nil {
		return windows.InvalidHandle, fmt.Errorf("encode open path: %w", err)
	}
	handle, err := windows.CreateFile(
		pathUTF16,
		spec.access,
		spec.share,
		nil,
		spec.creation,
		spec.options,
		0,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return windows.InvalidHandle, fmt.Errorf("create file: %w", err)
	}
	return handle, nil
}

type ntUnicodeString struct {
	length        uint16
	maximumLength uint16
	buffer        *uint16
}

type ntObjectAttributes struct {
	length                   uint32
	rootDirectory            windows.Handle
	objectName               *ntUnicodeString
	attributes               uint32
	securityDescriptor       uintptr
	securityQualityOfService uintptr
}

type ntIOStatusBlock struct {
	status      uintptr
	information uintptr
}

const (
	ntObjectCaseInsensitive  = uint32(0x00000040)
	ntFileOpen               = uint32(0x00000001)
	ntFileCreate             = uint32(0x00000002)
	ntFileOpenIf             = uint32(0x00000003)
	ntFileOverwrite          = uint32(0x00000004)
	ntFileOverwriteIf        = uint32(0x00000005)
	ntFileDirectoryFile      = uint32(0x00000001)
	ntFileNonDirectoryFile   = uint32(0x00000040)
	ntFileOpenReparsePoint   = uint32(0x00200000)
	fileAttributeNormal      = uint32(0x00000080)
	fileNameNormalized       = uint32(0)
	volumeNameDOS            = uint32(0)
	fileIDBothDirectoryInfo  = uint32(10)
	fileIDBothDirectoryStart = uint32(11)
	fileBasicInfoClass       = uint32(0)
	fileStandardInfoClass    = uint32(1)
	fileIDInfoClass          = uint32(18)
	fileDispositionInfoClass = uint32(4)
	fileDispositionExClass   = uint32(21)
	fileRenameInfoExClass    = uint32(22)
	fileCaseSensitiveClass   = uint32(23)
	fileDispositionDelete    = uint32(0x00000001)
	fileRenameReplace        = uint32(0x00000001)
	fileCaseSensitiveDir     = uint32(0x00000001)
)

func openRelativeWindows(
	parent windows.Handle,
	name string,
	spec openSpec,
) (windows.Handle, error) {
	if name == "" || name == "." || name == ".." ||
		strings.ContainsAny(name, `/\`) {
		return windows.InvalidHandle, fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid relative leaf", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	nameUTF16, err := windows.UTF16FromString(name)
	if err != nil {
		return windows.InvalidHandle, fmt.Errorf("encode relative leaf: %w", err)
	}
	nameBytes := (len(nameUTF16) - 1) * 2
	if nameBytes > math.MaxUint16 {
		return windows.InvalidHandle, fmt.Errorf("%w: relative leaf is too long", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	unicodeName := ntUnicodeString{
		length:        uint16(nameBytes),
		maximumLength: uint16(len(nameUTF16) * 2),
		buffer:        &nameUTF16[0],
	}
	attributes := ntObjectAttributes{
		length:        uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(ntObjectAttributes{})),
		rootDirectory: parent,
		objectName:    &unicodeName,
		attributes:    ntObjectCaseInsensitive,
	}
	disposition, err := ntDisposition(spec.creation)
	if err != nil {
		return windows.InvalidHandle, err
	}
	options := ntFileNonDirectoryFile
	if spec.directory {
		options = ntFileDirectoryFile
	}
	if spec.options&windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT != 0 {
		options |= ntFileOpenReparsePoint
	}

	var handle windows.Handle
	var status ntIOStatusBlock
	procedure := windows.NewLazySystemDLL("ntdll.dll").NewProc("NtCreateFile")
	result, _, _ := procedure.Call(
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&handle)),
		uintptr(spec.access),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&attributes)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&status)),
		0,
		uintptr(fileAttributeNormal),
		uintptr(spec.share),
		uintptr(disposition),
		uintptr(options),
		0,
		0,
	)
	if ntStatus := uint32(result); ntStatus != 0 {
		return windows.InvalidHandle, fmt.Errorf("nt create file: %w", ntStatusError(ntStatus))
	}
	return handle, nil
}

func ntDisposition(creation uint32) (uint32, error) {
	switch creation {
	case windows.CREATE_NEW:
		return ntFileCreate, nil
	case windows.CREATE_ALWAYS:
		return ntFileOverwriteIf, nil
	case windows.OPEN_EXISTING:
		return ntFileOpen, nil
	case windows.OPEN_ALWAYS:
		return ntFileOpenIf, nil
	case windows.TRUNCATE_EXISTING:
		return ntFileOverwrite, nil
	default:
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("%w: unsupported creation disposition", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
}

func ntStatusError(status uint32) error {
	procedure := windows.NewLazySystemDLL("ntdll.dll").NewProc("RtlNtStatusToDosError")
	code, _, _ := procedure.Call(uintptr(status))
	if code == 0 {
		return syscall.Errno(status)
	}
	return syscall.Errno(code)
}

func finalPathWindows(handle windows.Handle) (string, error) {
	procedure := windows.NewLazySystemDLL("kernel32.dll").
		NewProc("GetFinalPathNameByHandleW")
	buffer := make([]uint16, 512)
	for {
		length, _, callErr := procedure.Call(
			uintptr(handle),
			uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&buffer[0])),
			uintptr(len(buffer)),
			uintptr(fileNameNormalized|volumeNameDOS),
		)
		if length == 0 {
			if errors.Is(callErr, windows.ERROR_SUCCESS) {
				callErr = errors.New("get final path returned no data")
			}
			return "", fmt.Errorf("get final path: %w", callErr)
		}
		if length < uintptr(len(buffer)) {
			return windows.UTF16ToString(buffer[:length]), nil
		}
		buffer = make([]uint16, int(length)+1)
	}
}

type fileBasicInfo struct {
	creationTime   int64
	lastAccessTime int64
	lastWriteTime  int64
	changeTime     int64
	attributes     uint32
	_              uint32
}

type fileStandardInfo struct {
	allocationSize int64
	endOfFile      int64
	numberOfLinks  uint32
	deletePending  byte
	directory      byte
	_              [2]byte
}

type fileIDInfo struct {
	volumeSerial uint64
	fileID       [16]byte
}

type fileCaseSensitiveInfo struct {
	flags uint32
}

func identityWindows(handle windows.Handle) (objectIdentity, error) {
	var basic fileBasicInfo
	if err := getFileInformationWindows(
		handle,
		fileBasicInfoClass,
		unsafe.Pointer(&basic),
		unsafe.Sizeof(basic),
	); err != nil {
		return objectIdentity{}, fmt.Errorf("read basic file info: %w", err)
	}
	var standard fileStandardInfo
	if err := getFileInformationWindows(
		handle,
		fileStandardInfoClass,
		unsafe.Pointer(&standard),
		unsafe.Sizeof(standard),
	); err != nil {
		return objectIdentity{}, fmt.Errorf("read standard file info: %w", err)
	}
	var id fileIDInfo
	if err := getFileInformationWindows(
		handle,
		fileIDInfoClass,
		unsafe.Pointer(&id),
		unsafe.Sizeof(id),
	); err != nil {
		return objectIdentity{}, fmt.Errorf("read file id info: %w", err)
	}
	return objectIdentity{
		volumeSerial:  id.volumeSerial,
		fileID:        id.fileID,
		attributes:    basic.attributes,
		numberOfLinks: standard.numberOfLinks,
		size:          standard.endOfFile,
	}, nil
}

func getFileInformationWindows(
	handle windows.Handle,
	class uint32,
	information unsafe.Pointer,
	size uintptr,
) error {
	procedure := windows.NewLazySystemDLL("kernel32.dll").
		NewProc("GetFileInformationByHandleEx")
	result, _, callErr := procedure.Call(
		uintptr(handle),
		uintptr(class),
		uintptr(information),
		size,
	)
	if result == 0 {
		if errors.Is(callErr, windows.ERROR_SUCCESS) {
			callErr = errors.New("get file information returned false")
		}
		return callErr
	}
	return nil
}

func caseSensitiveWindows(handle windows.Handle) (bool, error) {
	var information fileCaseSensitiveInfo
	if err := getFileInformationWindows(
		handle,
		fileCaseSensitiveClass,
		unsafe.Pointer(&information),
		unsafe.Sizeof(information),
	); err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("read case-sensitive info: %w", err)
	}
	return information.flags&fileCaseSensitiveDir != 0, nil
}

func driveTypeWindows(root string) (uint32, error) {
	rootUTF16, err := windows.UTF16PtrFromString(root)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("encode volume root: %w", err)
	}
	procedure := windows.NewLazySystemDLL("kernel32.dll").NewProc("GetDriveTypeW")
	result, _, _ := procedure.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(rootUTF16)))
	driveType := uint32(result)
	if driveType == windows.DRIVE_UNKNOWN || driveType == windows.DRIVE_NO_ROOT_DIR {
		return 0, errors.New("get drive type returned an unusable volume")
	}
	return driveType, nil
}

func duplicateHandleWindows(handle windows.Handle) (windows.Handle, error) {
	process := windows.CurrentProcess()
	var duplicate windows.Handle
	if err := windows.DuplicateHandle(
		process,
		handle,
		process,
		&duplicate,
		0,
		false,
		windows.DUPLICATE_SAME_ACCESS,
	); err != nil {
		return windows.InvalidHandle, fmt.Errorf("duplicate handle: %w", err)
	}
	return duplicate, nil
}

type fileIDBothDirectoryInformation struct {
	nextEntryOffset uint32
	fileIndex       uint32
	creationTime    int64
	lastAccessTime  int64
	lastWriteTime   int64
	changeTime      int64
	endOfFile       int64
	allocationSize  int64
	fileAttributes  uint32
	fileNameLength  uint32
	eaSize          uint32
	shortNameLength byte
	_               byte
	shortName       [12]uint16
	fileID          int64
	fileName        [1]uint16
}

func listDirectoryWindows(handle windows.Handle) ([]directoryEntry, error) {
	buffer := make([]byte, 64*1024)
	class := fileIDBothDirectoryStart
	entries := make([]directoryEntry, 0, 16)
	for {
		err := getFileInformationWindows(
			handle,
			class,
			unsafe.Pointer(&buffer[0]),
			uintptr(len(buffer)),
		)
		if errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_NO_MORE_FILES) {
			return entries, nil
		}
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("list directory: %w", err)
		}
		class = fileIDBothDirectoryInfo

		offset := uint32(0)
		for {
			if int(offset)+int(unsafe.Sizeof(fileIDBothDirectoryInformation{})) > len(buffer) {
				return nil, errors.New("list directory returned a truncated entry")
			}
			information := (*fileIDBothDirectoryInformation)(
				unsafe.Pointer(&buffer[offset]),
			)
			nameUnits := int(information.fileNameLength / 2)
			nameOffset := int(offset) + int(unsafe.Offsetof(information.fileName))
			nameEnd := nameOffset + nameUnits*2
			if information.fileNameLength%2 != 0 || nameEnd > len(buffer) {
				return nil, errors.New("list directory returned an invalid name")
			}
			namePointer := (*uint16)(unsafe.Pointer(&buffer[nameOffset]))
			name := windows.UTF16ToString(unsafe.Slice(namePointer, nameUnits))
			if name != "." && name != ".." {
				entries = append(entries, directoryEntry{
					name:       name,
					attributes: information.fileAttributes,
				})
			}
			if information.nextEntryOffset == 0 {
				break
			}
			if information.nextEntryOffset%4 != 0 {
				return nil, errors.New("list directory returned an invalid offset")
			}
			offset += information.nextEntryOffset
			if int(offset) >= len(buffer) {
				return nil, errors.New("list directory offset exceeds buffer")
			}
		}
	}
}

func readFileWindows(handle windows.Handle, buffer []byte) (int, error) {
	var read uint32
	if err := windows.ReadFile(handle, buffer, &read, nil); err != nil {
		return int(read), fmt.Errorf("read file: %w", err)
	}
	return int(read), nil
}

func writeFileWindows(handle windows.Handle, buffer []byte) (int, error) {
	var written uint32
	if err := windows.WriteFile(handle, buffer, &written, nil); err != nil {
		return int(written), fmt.Errorf("write file: %w", err)
	}
	return int(written), nil
}

type fileDispositionInfo struct {
	deleteFile byte
}

type fileDispositionInfoEx struct {
	flags uint32
}

func setDispositionWindows(handle windows.Handle) error {
	extended := fileDispositionInfoEx{flags: fileDispositionDelete}
	err := setFileInformationWindows(
		handle,
		fileDispositionExClass,
		unsafe.Pointer(&extended),
		uintptr(unsafe.Sizeof(extended)),
	)
	if err == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if !errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER) {
		return fmt.Errorf("set disposition ex: %w", err)
	}
	legacy := fileDispositionInfo{deleteFile: 1}
	if err := setFileInformationWindows(
		handle,
		fileDispositionInfoClass,
		unsafe.Pointer(&legacy),
		uintptr(unsafe.Sizeof(legacy)),
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("set disposition: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

type fileRenameInfoEx struct {
	flags          uint32
	rootDirectory  windows.Handle
	fileNameLength uint32
	fileName       [1]uint16
}

func renameWindows(
	source windows.Handle,
	targetParent windows.Handle,
	name string,
	replace bool,
) error {
	nameUTF16, err := windows.UTF16FromString(name)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("encode rename destination: %w", err)
	}
	nameUTF16 = nameUTF16[:len(nameUTF16)-1]
	nameBytes := len(nameUTF16) * 2
	offset := int(unsafe.Offsetof(fileRenameInfoEx{}.fileName))
	buffer := make([]byte, offset+nameBytes)
	information := (*fileRenameInfoEx)(unsafe.Pointer(&buffer[0]))
	if replace {
		information.flags = fileRenameReplace
	}
	information.rootDirectory = targetParent
	information.fileNameLength = uint32(nameBytes)
	copy(unsafe.Slice(&information.fileName[0], len(nameUTF16)), nameUTF16)
	if err := setFileInformationWindows(
		source,
		fileRenameInfoExClass,
		unsafe.Pointer(information),
		uintptr(len(buffer)),
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("rename by handle: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func setFileInformationWindows(
	handle windows.Handle,
	class uint32,
	information unsafe.Pointer,
	size uintptr,
) error {
	procedure := windows.NewLazySystemDLL("kernel32.dll").
		NewProc("SetFileInformationByHandle")
	result, _, callErr := procedure.Call(
		uintptr(handle),
		uintptr(class),
		uintptr(information),
		size,
	)
	if result == 0 {
		if errors.Is(callErr, windows.ERROR_SUCCESS) {
			callErr = errors.New("set file information returned false")
		}
		return callErr
	}
	return nil
}
```

`fileIDBothDirectoryInformation`、`fileDispositionInfoEx`、`fileRenameInfoEx` 的
offset/size，且 `openRelativeWindows` 的 RootDirectory 必须是传入 parent handle。

- [ ] **步骤 1：编写固定名称的句柄安全测试**

将以下完整函数与 helper 追加到 `path_windows_test.go`，并把 `context` 与
`internal/config` 合并进该文件现有 import：

```go
func TestPinnedChain_RejectsReparsePointAtEveryLevel(t *testing.T) {
	parent := t.TempDir()
	external := t.TempDir()
	link := filepath.Join(parent, "linked")
	if err := os.Symlink(external, link); err != nil {
		t.Skipf("directory symlink unavailable: %v", err)
	}
	rootParent := mustCanonicalize(t, parent)
	target := mustCanonicalize(t, link)
	chain, err := openPinnedChainWith(
		t.Context(),
		rootParent,
		target,
		directoryPinSpec(),
		newProductionPathAPI(),
	)
	if chain != nil {
		_ = chain.close()
		t.Fatal("openPinnedChainWith() returned a chain through a reparse point")
	}
	assertFilesystemCode(t, err, protocol.CodeUnsafeReparsePoint)
}

func TestPinnedChain_RejectsIdentityOrParentMismatch(t *testing.T) {
	chain := openPinnedTestChain(t)
	defer func() {
		if err := chain.close(); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("chain.close() error = %v", err)
		}
	}()
	parent := chain.objects[len(chain.objects)-2]
	child := chain.objects[len(chain.objects)-1]
	parent.identity.fileID[0] ^= 0xff
	err := validateParentIdentityWith(t.Context(), parent, child, chain.api)
	if !errors.Is(err, ErrIdentityChanged) {
		t.Fatalf("validateParentIdentityWith() error = %v, want ErrIdentityChanged", err)
	}
}

func TestPinnedChain_HoldsEveryAncestorUntilClose(t *testing.T) {
	chain := openPinnedTestChain(t)
	target := chain.objects[len(chain.objects)-1].path.String()
	replacement := target + "-renamed"
	if err := os.Rename(target, replacement); err == nil {
		_ = os.Rename(replacement, target)
		_ = chain.close()
		t.Fatal("os.Rename() succeeded while the target was pinned")
	}
	if err := chain.close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("chain.close() error = %v", err)
	}
	if err := os.Rename(target, replacement); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.Rename() after close error = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestDangerousRootValidation_RejectsUNCAndRemoteRoots(t *testing.T) {
	api := newProductionPathAPI()
	api.driveType = func(string) (uint32, error) {
		return windows.DRIVE_REMOTE, nil
	}
	path := mustCanonicalize(t, t.TempDir())
	err := ensureLocalVolumeWith(path, api)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("ensureLocalVolumeWith() error = nil, want remote-volume rejection")
	}

	unc := canonicalPathForTest(`\\server\share\AUTO-MAS`)
	if err := ensureLocalVolumeWith(unc, api); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("ensureLocalVolumeWith(UNC) error = nil, want rejection")
	}
}

func TestDangerousRootValidation_RejectsCaseSensitiveAncestor(t *testing.T) {
	api := newProductionPathAPI()
	api.caseSensitive = func(windows.Handle) (bool, error) { return true, nil }
	parent := t.TempDir()
	target := filepath.Join(parent, "app")
	if err := os.Mkdir(target, 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.Mkdir() error = %v", err)
	}
	chain, err := openPinnedChainWith(
		t.Context(),
		mustCanonicalize(t, parent),
		mustCanonicalize(t, target),
		directoryPinSpec(),
		api,
	)
	if chain != nil {
		_ = chain.close()
		t.Fatal("openPinnedChainWith() returned a case-sensitive chain")
	}
	if !errors.Is(err, ErrUnsupportedCaseSensitivity) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want ErrUnsupportedCaseSensitivity", err)
	}
}

func TestDangerousRootValidation_RejectsIndeterminateCaseSensitivity(t *testing.T) {
	injected := errors.New("case query failed")
	api := newProductionPathAPI()
	api.caseSensitive = func(windows.Handle) (bool, error) {
		return false, injected
	}
	parent := t.TempDir()
	target := filepath.Join(parent, "app")
	if err := os.Mkdir(target, 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.Mkdir() error = %v", err)
	}
	chain, err := openPinnedChainWith(
		t.Context(),
		mustCanonicalize(t, parent),
		mustCanonicalize(t, target),
		directoryPinSpec(),
		api,
	)
	if chain != nil {
		_ = chain.close()
		t.Fatal("openPinnedChainWith() returned an indeterminate chain")
	}
	if !errors.Is(err, injected) ||
		!errors.Is(err, ErrUnsupportedCaseSensitivity) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want injected and unsupported-case errors", err)
	}
}

func TestDangerousRootValidation_RejectsNilContextAndLayout(t *testing.T) {
	layout := newPathTestLayout(t)
	api := newProductionPathAPI()
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		ctx    context.Context
		layout *config.Layout
	}{
		{name: "nil context", layout: layout},
		{name: "nil layout", ctx: t.Context()},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			chains, err := validateDangerousRootWith(
				test.ctx,
				test.layout,
				[]string{layout.AppRoot()},
				api,
			)
			if chains != nil {
				t.Fatalf("chains = %#v, want nil", chains)
			}
			if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument) {
				t.Fatalf("error = %v, want ErrInvalidArgument", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
```
