```go
func TestStateFiles_WriteAtomicRejectsEmptyAndOversizeBeforeIO(t *testing.T) {
	files, _ := newStateFixture(t)
	openCalls := 0
	openRelative := files.api.openRelative
	files.api.openRelative = func(
		parent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		spec openSpec,
	) (windows.Handle, error) {
		openCalls++
		return openRelative(parent, name, spec)
	}
	for _, payload := range [][]byte{
		nil,
		{},
		bytes.Repeat([]byte{'x'}, int(MaxStateFileBytes)+1),
	} {
		if result, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, payload); result.MutationApplied ||
			!errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument) {
			t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic(len=%d) = %#v, %v", len(payload), result, err)
		}
	}
	if openCalls != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("open calls = %d, want 0", openCalls)
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_WriteAtomicCopiesCallerPayload(t *testing.T) {
	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
	payload := []byte("payload")
	result, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, payload)
	if err != nil || !result.MutationApplied {
		t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic() = %#v, %v", result, err)
	}
	payload[0] = 'X'
	got, err := os.ReadFile(layout.BackendStateFile())
	if err != nil || string(got) != "payload" {
		t.Fatalf("published payload = %q, error = %v", got, err)
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_WriteAtomicReturnsTypedStateWriteErrors(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name        string
		phase       StateWritePhase
		wantApplied bool
		wantCleanup bool
		wantCause   error
		inject      func(*pathAPI, string, error, error)
	}{
		{
			name:  "create temp",
			phase: StateWritePhaseCreate,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, _ string, injected, _ error) {
				openRelative := api.openRelative
				api.openRelative = func(
					parent windows.Handle,
					name string,
					spec openSpec,
				) (windows.Handle, error) {
					if spec.creation == windows.CREATE_NEW {
						return windows.InvalidHandle, injected
					}
					return openRelative(parent, name, spec)
				}
			},
		},
		{
			name:  "write",
			phase: StateWritePhaseWrite,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, _ string, injected, _ error) {
				api.writeFile = func(windows.Handle, []byte) (int, error) {
					return 0, injected
				}
			},
		},
		{
			name:  "sync",
			phase: StateWritePhaseSync,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, _ string, injected, _ error) {
				api.flushFile = func(windows.Handle) error { return injected }
			},
		},
		{
			name:  "temp identity",
			phase: StateWritePhaseRename,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, _ string, injected, _ error) {
				identity := api.identity
				seen := make(map[windows.Handle]int)
				api.identity = func(handle windows.Handle) (objectIdentity, error) {
					got, err := identity(handle)
					if err == nil &&
						got.attributes&windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY == 0 {
						seen[handle]++
						if seen[handle] == 2 {
							return objectIdentity{}, injected
						}
					}
					return got, err
				}
			},
		},
		{
			name:  "destination inspection",
			phase: StateWritePhaseRename,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, path string, injected, _ error) {
				openRelative := api.openRelative
				api.openRelative = func(
					parent windows.Handle,
					name string,
					spec openSpec,
				) (windows.Handle, error) {
					if name == filepath.Base(path) &&
						spec.creation == windows.OPEN_EXISTING &&
						!spec.directory {
						return windows.InvalidHandle, injected
					}
					return openRelative(parent, name, spec)
				}
			},
		},
		{
			name:  "destination identity",
			phase: StateWritePhaseRename,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, path string, injected, _ error) {
				openRelative := api.openRelative
				identity := api.identity
				destinationHandle := windows.InvalidHandle
				api.openRelative = func(
					parent windows.Handle,
					name string,
					spec openSpec,
				) (windows.Handle, error) {
					if name == filepath.Base(path) &&
						spec.creation == windows.OPEN_EXISTING &&
						!spec.directory {
						handle, err := openRelative(parent, name, spec)
						if err == nil {
							destinationHandle = handle
						}
						return handle, err
					}
					return openRelative(parent, name, spec)
				}
				api.identity = func(handle windows.Handle) (objectIdentity, error) {
					if handle == destinationHandle {
						return objectIdentity{}, injected
					}
					return identity(handle)
				}
			},
		},
		{
			name:      "destination hard-link safety",
			phase:     StateWritePhaseRename,
			wantCause: ErrUnsafeHardLink,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, path string, _, _ error) {
				openRelative := api.openRelative
				identity := api.identity
				destinationHandle := windows.InvalidHandle
				api.openRelative = func(
					parent windows.Handle,
					name string,
					spec openSpec,
				) (windows.Handle, error) {
					handle, err := openRelative(parent, name, spec)
					if err == nil &&
						name == filepath.Base(path) &&
						spec.creation == windows.OPEN_EXISTING &&
						!spec.directory {
						destinationHandle = handle
					}
					return handle, err
				}
				api.identity = func(handle windows.Handle) (objectIdentity, error) {
					got, err := identity(handle)
					if err == nil && handle == destinationHandle {
						got.numberOfLinks = 2
					}
					return got, err
				}
			},
		},
		{
			name:  "rename",
			phase: StateWritePhaseRename,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, _ string, injected, _ error) {
				api.rename = func(
					windows.Handle,
					windows.Handle,
					string,
					bool,
				) error {
					return injected
				}
			},
		},
		{
			name:        "cleanup",
			phase:       StateWritePhaseWrite,
			wantCleanup: true,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, _ string, injected, cleanup error) {
				api.writeFile = func(windows.Handle, []byte) (int, error) {
					return 0, injected
				}
				api.setDisposition = func(windows.Handle) error {
					return cleanup
				}
			},
		},
		{
			name:        "post-rename close",
			phase:       StateWritePhaseClose,
			wantApplied: true,
			inject: func(api *pathAPI, _ string, injected, _ error) {
				rename := api.rename
				closeHandle := api.closeHandle
				published := false
				api.rename = func(
					source windows.Handle,
					parent windows.Handle,
					name string,
					replace bool,
				) error {
					err := rename(source, parent, name, replace)
					published = err == nil
					return err
				}
				api.closeHandle = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
					err := closeHandle(handle)
					if published {
						published = false
						return errors.Join(injected, err)
					}
					return err
				}
			},
		},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
			path := layout.BackendStateFile()
			if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte("old"), 0o600); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
			}
			injected := errors.New("injected " + test.name)
			cleanupInjected := errors.New("injected cleanup")
			api := files.api
			test.inject(&api, path, injected, cleanupInjected)
			files.api = api
			result, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, []byte("new"))
			var writeErr *StateWriteError
			if !errors.As(err, &writeErr) {
				t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic() error = %v, want *StateWriteError", err)
			}
			if result.MutationApplied != test.wantApplied ||
				writeErr.MutationApplied != test.wantApplied ||
				writeErr.Phase != test.phase {
				t.Fatalf(
					"WriteAtomic() = %#v, %#v, want phase %q/applied %t",
					result,
					writeErr,
					test.phase,
					test.wantApplied,
				)
			}
			wantCause := test.wantCause
			if wantCause == nil {
				wantCause = injected
			}
			if !errors.Is(writeErr.Cause, wantCause) ||
				!errors.Is(err, wantCause) {
				t.Fatalf("Cause = %v, want primary error %v", writeErr.Cause, wantCause)
			}
			var fileErr *FileError
			if !errors.As(writeErr.Cause, &fileErr) {
				t.Fatalf("Cause = %v, want low-level *FileError", writeErr.Cause)
			}
			if test.wantCleanup {
				if !errors.Is(writeErr.CleanupError, cleanupInjected) ||
					!errors.Is(err, cleanupInjected) {
					t.Fatalf(
						"CleanupError = %v, want injected cleanup",
						writeErr.CleanupError,
					)
				}
				if errors.Is(writeErr.Cause, cleanupInjected) ||
					errors.Is(writeErr.CleanupError, injected) {
					t.Fatal("primary and cleanup causes were mixed")
				}
			} else if writeErr.CleanupError != nil {
				t.Fatalf("CleanupError = %v, want nil", writeErr.CleanupError)
			}
			got, readErr := os.ReadFile(path)
			want := "old"
			if test.wantApplied {
				want = "new"
			}
			if readErr != nil || string(got) != want {
				t.Fatalf("destination = %q, error = %v, want %q", got, readErr, want)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_WriteAtomicNeverReplacesUnsafeDestination(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("hard link", func(t *testing.T) {
		files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
		path := layout.BackendStateFile()
		if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte("old"), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
		}
		alias := path + ".alias"
		if err := os.Link(path, alias); err != nil {
			t.Skipf("hard-link fixture unavailable: %v", err)
		}
		result, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, []byte("new"))
		if result.MutationApplied || !errors.Is(err, ErrUnsafeHardLink) {
			t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic() = %#v, %v, want unsafe rejection", result, err)
		}
		assertStateWritePhase(t, err, StateWritePhaseRename, false)
		got, _ := os.ReadFile(alias)
		if string(got) != "old" {
			t.Fatalf("hard-link target = %q, want old", got)
		}
	})
	t.Run("reparse", func(t *testing.T) {
		files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
		external := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "external.json")
		if err := os.WriteFile(external, []byte("external"), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
		}
		if err := os.Symlink(external, layout.BackendStateFile()); err != nil {
			t.Skipf("file symlink unavailable: %v", err)
		}
		result, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, []byte("new"))
		if result.MutationApplied || err == nil {
			t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic() = %#v, %v, want reparse rejection", result, err)
		}
		assertStateWritePhase(t, err, StateWritePhaseRename, false)
		got, _ := os.ReadFile(external)
		if string(got) != "external" {
			t.Fatalf("external target = %q, want unchanged", got)
		}
	})
}

func TestStateFiles_WriteAtomicRejectsReparseDestination(t *testing.T) {
	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
	external := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "external.json")
	if err := os.WriteFile(external, []byte("external"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
	}
	if err := os.Symlink(external, layout.BackendStateFile()); err != nil {
		t.Skipf("file symlink unavailable: %v", err)
	}
	if result, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, []byte("new")); result.MutationApplied || err == nil {
		t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic() = %#v, %v, want rejection", result, err)
	} else {
		assertStateWritePhase(t, err, StateWritePhaseRename, false)

}

func TestStateFiles_WriteAtomicRejectsHardLinkDestination(t *testing.T) {
	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
	if err := os.WriteFile(layout.BackendStateFile(), []byte("old"), 0o600); err != nil {

func TestStateFiles_WriteAtomicReportsAppliedBeforeCloseError(t *testing.T) {
	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
	injected := errors.New("post-rename close failed")
	rename := files.api.rename
	closeHandle := files.api.closeHandle
	renamed := false
	files.api.rename = func(
		source windows.Handle,
		parent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		replace bool,
	) error {
		err := rename(source, parent, name, replace)
		if err == nil {
			renamed = true
		}
		return err
	}
	files.api.closeHandle = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		err := closeHandle(handle)
		if renamed {
			renamed = false
			return errors.Join(injected, err)
		}
		return err
	}
	result, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, []byte("new"))
	if !result.MutationApplied || !errors.Is(err, injected) {
		t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic() = %#v, %v, want applied/injected", result, err)
	}
	var writeErr *StateWriteError
	if !errors.As(err, &writeErr) ||
		writeErr.Phase != StateWritePhaseClose ||
		!writeErr.MutationApplied ||
		!errors.Is(writeErr.Cause, injected) ||
		writeErr.CleanupError != nil {
		t.Fatalf("StateWriteError = %#v, want close/applied/cause/no-cleanup", writeErr)
	}
	got, readErr := os.ReadFile(layout.BackendStateFile())
	if readErr != nil || string(got) != "new" {
		t.Fatalf("destination = %q, error = %v", got, readErr)
	}
}

func assertStateWritePhase(
	t *testing.T,
	err error,
	want StateWritePhase,
	wantApplied bool,
) {
	t.Helper()
	var writeErr *StateWriteError
	if !errors.As(err, &writeErr) ||
		writeErr.Phase != want ||
		writeErr.MutationApplied != wantApplied {
		t.Fatalf(
			"StateWriteError = %#v, want phase %q/applied %t",
			writeErr,
			want,
			wantApplied,
		)
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_RemoveTransactionRejectsInvalidSnapshot(t *testing.T) {

		!errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidToken) {

func TestStateFiles_RemoveTransactionDetectsReplacementAndRewrite(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		mutate func(string) error
	}{
		{
			name: "replacement",
			mutate: func(path string) error {
				if err := os.Remove(path); err != nil {
					return err
				}
				return os.WriteFile(path, []byte("replacement"), 0o600)
			},
		},
		{
			name: "rewrite",
			mutate: func(path string) error {
				return os.WriteFile(path, []byte("rewritten"), 0o600)
			},
		},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
			path := layout.MutationStateFile()
			if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte("original"), 0o600); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
			}
			snapshot, err := files.Read(t.Context(), StateMutation, MaxStateFileBytes)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("Read() error = %v", err)
			}
			if err := test.mutate(path); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("mutate() error = %v", err)
			}
			result, err := files.RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged(t.Context(), snapshot)
			if result.MutationApplied || !errors.Is(err, ErrIdentityChanged) {
				t.Fatalf("RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged() = %#v, %v", result, err)
			}
			if _, err := os.Stat(path); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("current state was removed: %v", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_RemoveTransactionComparesAndDeletesOnSameHandle(t *testing.T) {
	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
	path := layout.MutationStateFile()
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte("state"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
	}
	snapshot, err := files.Read(t.Context(), StateMutation, MaxStateFileBytes)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Read() error = %v", err)
	}
	entered := make(chan struct{})
	release := make(chan struct{})
	setDisposition := files.api.setDisposition
	files.api.setDisposition = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		close(entered)
		<-release
		return setDisposition(handle)
	}
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		_, err := files.RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged(t.Context(), snapshot)
		done <- err
	}()
	<-entered
	if err := os.Remove(path); err == nil {
		close(release)
		t.Fatal("competing remove succeeded while conditional leaf handle was held")
	}
	close(release)
	if err := <-done; err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged() error = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_RemoveTransactionReportsAppliedBeforeCloseError(t *testing.T) {
	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
	path := layout.MutationStateFile()
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte("state"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
	}
	snapshot, err := files.Read(t.Context(), StateMutation, MaxStateFileBytes)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Read() error = %v", err)
	}
	injected := errors.New("close failed")
	closeHandle := files.api.closeHandle
	disposed := false
	setDisposition := files.api.setDisposition
	files.api.setDisposition = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		err := setDisposition(handle)
		disposed = err == nil
		return err
	}
	files.api.closeHandle = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		err := closeHandle(handle)
		if disposed {
			disposed = false
			return errors.Join(injected, err)
		}
		return err
	}
	result, err := files.RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged(t.Context(), snapshot)
	if !result.MutationApplied || !errors.Is(err, injected) {
		t.Fatalf("RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged() = %#v, %v", result, err)
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_WriteAndRemoveRejectAfterClose(t *testing.T) {
	files, _ := newStateFixture(t)
	if err := files.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Close() error = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, []byte("state")); !errors.Is(err, ErrClosed) {
		t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic() error = %v, want ErrClosed", err)
	}
	if _, err := files.RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged(t.Context(), StateFileSnapshot{}); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidToken) {
		t.Fatalf("RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged() error = %v, want ErrInvalidToken", err)
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_WriteAndRemoveContextsRejectedBeforeIO(t *testing.T) {
	files, _ := newStateFixture(t)
	ioCalls := 0
	unexpectedIO := errors.New("unexpected filesystem I/O")
	files.api.openRelative = func(
		windows.Handle,
		string,
		openSpec,
	) (windows.Handle, error) {
		ioCalls++
		return windows.InvalidHandle, unexpectedIO
	}
	files.api.identity = func(windows.Handle) (objectIdentity, error) {
		ioCalls++
		return objectIdentity{}, unexpectedIO
	}
	files.api.writeFile = func(windows.Handle, []byte) (int, error) {
		ioCalls++
		return 0, unexpectedIO
	}
	files.api.flushFile = func(windows.Handle) error {
		ioCalls++
		return unexpectedIO
	}
	files.api.setDisposition = func(windows.Handle) error {
		ioCalls++
		return unexpectedIO
	}
	files.api.rename = func(
		windows.Handle,
		windows.Handle,
		string,
		bool,
	) error {
		ioCalls++
		return unexpectedIO
	}

	canceledCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	cancel()
	deadlineCtx, deadlineCancel := context.WithTimeout(t.Context(), 0)
	defer deadlineCancel()
	contexts := []struct {
		name string
		ctx  context.Context
		want error
	}{
		{name: "nil", ctx: nil, want: ErrInvalidArgument},
		{name: "canceled", ctx: canceledCtx, want: context.Canceled},
		{name: "deadline", ctx: deadlineCtx, want: context.DeadlineExceeded},
	}
	operations := []struct {
		name           string
		call           func(context.Context) (bool, error)
		wantRawContext bool
	}{
		{
			name: "write",
			call: func(ctx context.Context) (bool, error) {
				result, err := files.WriteAtomic(ctx, StateBackend, []byte("state"))
				return result.MutationApplied, err
			},
			wantRawContext: true,
		},
		{
			name: "remove",
			call: func(ctx context.Context) (bool, error) {
				result, err := files.RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged(
					ctx,
					StateFileSnapshot{},
				)
				return result.MutationApplied, err
			},
		},
	}
	for _, operation := range operations {
		for _, contextCase := range contexts {
			t.Run(operation.name+"/"+contextCase.name, func(t *testing.T) {
				before := ioCalls
				applied, err := operation.call(contextCase.ctx)
				if applied || !errors.Is(err, contextCase.want) {
					t.Fatalf(
						"call() = applied %t, error %v, want false/%v",
						applied,
						err,
						contextCase.want,
					)
				}
				if operation.wantRawContext {
					var writeErr *StateWriteError
					if errors.As(err, &writeErr) {
						t.Fatalf(
							"call() error = %#v, want raw pre-I/O error",
							writeErr,
						)
					}
				}
				if ioCalls != before {
					t.Fatalf(
						"filesystem I/O calls = %d, want %d",
						ioCalls,
						before,
					)
				}
			})
		}
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_WriteAndRemoveMatchAccessMatrixAndParentIdentity(t *testing.T) {
	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
	var specs []openSpec
	openRelative := files.api.openRelative
	files.api.openRelative = func(
		parent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		spec openSpec,
	) (windows.Handle, error) {
		specs = append(specs, spec)
		return openRelative(parent, name, spec)
	}
	if _, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateMutation, []byte("state")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic() error = %v", err)
	}
	snapshot, err := files.Read(t.Context(), StateMutation, MaxStateFileBytes)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Read() error = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := files.RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged(t.Context(), snapshot); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged() error = %v", err)
	}
	if len(specs) < 3 {
		t.Fatalf("open specs = %#v, want temp/destination/remove coverage", specs)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(layout.MutationStateFile()); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		t.Fatalf("mutation state still exists: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestStateFiles_WriteDestinationMatchesAccessMatrixAndParentIdentity(t *testing.T) {
	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
	if err := os.WriteFile(layout.BackendStateFile(), []byte("old"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
	}
	var destinationSpec openSpec
	openRelative := files.api.openRelative
	files.api.openRelative = func(
		parent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		spec openSpec,
	) (windows.Handle, error) {
		if name == filepath.Base(layout.BackendStateFile()) {
			destinationSpec = spec
		}
		return openRelative(parent, name, spec)
	}
	if _, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, []byte("new")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("WriteAtomic() error = %v", err)
	}
	want := openSpec{
		access:    windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES | windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE | windows.FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
		creation:  windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
		options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
		directory: false,
	}
	if destinationSpec != want {
		t.Fatalf("destination openSpec = %#v, want %#v", destinationSpec, want)
	}
}

	files, _ := newStateFixture(t)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	rename := files.api.rename
	files.api.rename = func(
		source windows.Handle,
		parent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		replace bool,
	) error {
		err := rename(source, parent, name, replace)

	}
}

	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)

func TestWindows_StateAtomicReplaceKeepsOldAndNewReadersConsistent(t *testing.T) {
	files, _ := newStateFixture(t)
	if _, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, []byte("old")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("first WriteAtomic() error = %v", err)
	}
	oldSnapshot, err := files.Read(t.Context(), StateBackend, MaxStateFileBytes)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("old Read() error = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := files.WriteAtomic(t.Context(), StateBackend, []byte("new")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("second WriteAtomic() error = %v", err)
	}

		t.Fatalf("snapshots = %q/%q, want old/new", oldSnapshot.Bytes(), newSnapshot.Bytes())

func TestWindows_StateConditionalRemoveNeverDeletesReplacement(t *testing.T) {
	files, layout := newStateFixture(t)
	if err := os.WriteFile(layout.MutationStateFile(), []byte("old"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
	}
	snapshot, err := files.Read(t.Context(), StateMutation, MaxStateFileBytes)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Read() error = %v", err)
	}
	if err := os.Remove(layout.MutationStateFile()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.Remove() error = %v", err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(layout.MutationStateFile(), []byte("new"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile(new) error = %v", err)
	}
	if result, err := files.RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged(t.Context(), snapshot); result.MutationApplied ||
		!errors.Is(err, ErrIdentityChanged) {
		t.Fatalf("RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged() = %#v, %v", result, err)
	}
	got, _ := os.ReadFile(layout.MutationStateFile())
	if string(got) != "new" {
		t.Fatalf("replacement = %q, want new", got)
	}
}
```

`TestStateFiles_WriteAtomicReturnsTypedStateWriteErrors` 是跨包契约门，固定注入
create-temp、write、sync、temp identity、destination open / identity / hard-link safety、
rename、cleanup 和发布后 close 失败；每行都通过 `errors.As` 取得
`*StateWriteError`，断言 `Phase`、结果与错误内的 `MutationApplied` 一致，主错误只在
`Cause`，清理错误只在 `CleanupError`，且两条链都可由 `errors.Is` 到达。所有发布前
用例还断言旧正式文件逐字节不变，发布后 close 用例断言新 payload 已完整可见。

destination 对抗表分别放入文件 Reparse Point、目录 Reparse Point 和同卷硬链接，
必须在 rename 前返回 `StateWritePhaseRename` 的类型化错误，链接的另一个名称及外部
目标逐字节不变。rename 成功后 temp / destination inspection close 失败仍断言新
payload 已经发布、`MutationApplied=true`，且没有 disposition 正式文件。

conditional remove 分别在 snapshot 后执行同名 file ID 替换和同一 file ID 原地改写；
均返回 `ErrIdentityChanged`，当前对象逐字节不变。barrier 在 payload 比较完成后尝试替换
leaf，证明不授予 share-write/delete 的同一 handle 把比较与 disposition 线性化。

context 表对 WriteAtomic / RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged 传 nil、预取消和已超时
context，断言零 create/open/write/flush/rename/disposition；WriteAtomic 的三类预检错误
还必须证明不能 `errors.As` 为 `*StateWriteError`。两个提交点 barrier 分别在 rename 和
disposition 成功后取消 context；返回值仍保持 `MutationApplied=true`，取消不能触发
temp cleanup 或删除新 destination。权限测试逐字段断言 State atomic temp、正式
destination 安全检查和 conditional Remove 三行 `openSpec`；每行的 parent identity
不匹配时正式文件逐字节不变。

两个 Windows 对抗测试在真实 handles 上证明旧 reader 只见完整旧值、新 reader 只见
完整新值；snapshot 后同名替换与原地改写都不能被 conditional remove 删除。

- [ ] **步骤 2：运行红灯**

```powershell
Invoke-ExpectedFailure `
    "Test(StateFiles_(WriteAtomic|WriteDestination|RemoveTransaction|WriteAndRemove)|Windows_State)" `
    "(has no field or method WriteAtomic|has no field or method RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged|got MutationApplied false)"
```

- [ ] **步骤 3：实现 WriteAtomic**

持写锁后验证 open / kind / payload；同步消费调用方 slice，不存入 StateFiles。用固定
state-root handle 和保存的 `f.api` create-exclusive 随机临时 leaf，完整 write、
FlushFileBuffers、file ID 重验，然后按矩阵的“State 正式 destination 安全检查”行相对
打开正式文件。不存在可发布；存在时必须以该 handle 明确证明 ordinary、非 Reparse、
`NumberOfLinks == 1`、volume 与固定 state-root 一致，并通过
`validateParentIdentityWith(ctx, stateRoot, destination, f.api)`。Reparse 与 hard-link
分别包装 unsafe / `ErrUnsafeHardLink`，绝不进入 rename。inspection handle 保持到
rename 返回，再通过保存的 API 关闭。

`stateRoot := f.pins[1]`，`destinationPath` 来自 `statePath(f.layout, kind)`，
`destinationName := filepath.Base(destinationPath)`。发布只调用
`renameByHandleWith(temp.handle, stateRoot.handle, destinationName, true, f.api)`；
RootDirectory 为固定 state-root，flags 仅 replace-if-exists。

阶段映射固定为：create-temp 为 `StateWritePhaseCreate`，payload write 为
`StateWritePhaseWrite`，`FlushFileBuffers` 为 `StateWritePhaseSync`，发布前 temp
identity、destination 安全检查和 rename 全部为 `StateWritePhaseRename`，发布成功后
close 为 `StateWritePhaseClose`。低层 `FileError` 保留在 `Cause`；rename 前任何失败
都通过 `deleteByHandleWith(temp.handle, f.api)` 清理 temp，destination inspection
handle 的关闭错误与 temp disposition / close 错误只进入 `CleanupError`。

rename 成功立即冻结 `WriteAtomicResult{MutationApplied: true}`；随后 close 失败返回
同样 applied 的 `StateWriteError{Phase: StateWritePhaseClose}`，不得 disposition 或
删除已经发布的 destination。

完整实现如下：

```go
func (f *StateFiles) WriteAtomic(
	ctx context.Context,
	kind StateFileKind,
	payload []byte,
) (WriteAtomicResult, error) {
	if ctx == nil {
		return WriteAtomicResult{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: context is nil", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return WriteAtomicResult{}, err
	}
	if !kind.Valid() || len(payload) == 0 || int64(len(payload)) > MaxStateFileBytes {
		return WriteAtomicResult{}, ErrInvalidArgument
	}
	copied := append([]byte(nil), payload...)
	path, err := statePath(f.layout, kind)
	if err != nil {
		return WriteAtomicResult{}, err
	}

	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if f.closed {
		return WriteAtomicResult{}, ErrClosed
	}
	temp, err := f.createStateTemp(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return WriteAtomicResult{}, err
	}
	cleanup := func(
		phase StateWritePhase,
		cause error,
		priorCleanup error,
	) (WriteAtomicResult, error) {
		cleanupErr := errors.Join(
			priorCleanup,
			wrapFileError(
				"remove-temp",
				temp.path.String(),
				deleteByHandleWith(temp.handle, f.api),
			),
			closePinnedObject(f.api, &temp),
		)
		return WriteAtomicResult{}, &StateWriteError{
			Phase:           phase,
			MutationApplied: false,
			Cause:           cause,
			CleanupError:    cleanupErr,
		}
	}
	if err := writeAllHandle(ctx, temp.handle, copied, f.api); err != nil {
		return cleanup(
			StateWritePhaseWrite,
			&FileError{Operation: "write", Path: temp.path.String(), Err: err},
			nil,
		)
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return cleanup(
			StateWritePhaseSync,
			&FileError{Operation: "sync", Path: temp.path.String(), Err: err},
			nil,
		)
	}
	if err := f.api.flushFile(temp.handle); err != nil {
		return cleanup(
			StateWritePhaseSync,
			&FileError{Operation: "flush", Path: temp.path.String(), Err: err},
			nil,
		)
	}
	identity, err := f.api.identity(temp.handle)
	if err != nil {
		return cleanup(
			StateWritePhaseRename,
			&FileError{Operation: "identify", Path: temp.path.String(), Err: err},
			nil,
		)
	}
	if identity.volumeSerial != temp.identity.volumeSerial ||
		identity.fileID != temp.identity.fileID ||
		identity.numberOfLinks != 1 {
		return cleanup(
			StateWritePhaseRename,
			&FileError{
				Operation: "identify",
				Path:      temp.path.String(),
				Err:       ErrIdentityChanged,
			},
			nil,
		)
	}

	destination, destinationExists, err, inspectionCleanup :=
		f.inspectStateDestination(ctx, path)
	if err != nil {
		return cleanup(
			StateWritePhaseRename,
			&FileError{Operation: "inspect-destination", Path: path, Err: err},
			inspectionCleanup,
		)
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		if destinationExists {
			inspectionCleanup = errors.Join(
				inspectionCleanup,
				closePinnedObject(f.api, &destination),
			)
		}
		return cleanup(
			StateWritePhaseRename,
			&FileError{Operation: "rename", Path: path, Err: err},
			inspectionCleanup,
		)
	}
	if err := renameByHandleWith(
		temp.handle,
		f.pins[1].handle,
		filepath.Base(path),
		true,
		f.api,
	); err != nil {
		if destinationExists {
			inspectionCleanup = errors.Join(
				inspectionCleanup,
				closePinnedObject(f.api, &destination),
			)
		}
		return cleanup(
			StateWritePhaseRename,
			&FileError{Operation: "rename", Path: path, Err: err},
			inspectionCleanup,
		)
	}

	result := WriteAtomicResult{MutationApplied: true}
	closeErr := closePinnedObject(f.api, &temp)
	if destinationExists {
		closeErr = errors.Join(closeErr, closePinnedObject(f.api, &destination))
	}
	if closeErr != nil {
		return result, &StateWriteError{
			Phase:           StateWritePhaseClose,
			MutationApplied: true,
			Cause:           closeErr,
			CleanupError:    nil,
		}
	}
	return result, nil
}

func (f *StateFiles) createStateTemp(
	ctx context.Context,
) (pinnedObject, error) {
	stateDir := f.layout.StateDir()
	fail := func(cause, cleanup error) (pinnedObject, error) {
		return pinnedObject{}, &StateWriteError{
			Phase:           StateWritePhaseCreate,
			MutationApplied: false,
			Cause:           cause,
			CleanupError:    cleanup,
		}
	}
	spec := openSpec{
		access: windows.FILE_WRITE_DATA |
			windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES |
			windows.DELETE |
			windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ,
		creation:  windows.CREATE_NEW,
		options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
		directory: false,
	}
	for attempt := 0; attempt < 16; attempt++ {
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return fail(&FileError{
				Operation: "create-temp",
				Path:      stateDir,
				Err:       err,
			}, nil)
		}
		name, err := randomStateTempName()
		if err != nil {
			return fail(&FileError{
				Operation: "create-temp",
				Path:      stateDir,
				Err:       err,
			}, nil)
		}
		temp, err := openRelativeCheckedWith(ctx, f.pins[1], name, spec, f.api)
		if errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_FILE_EXISTS) ||
			errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS) {
			continue
		}
		if err != nil {
			cause, cleanup := splitOperationCleanup(err)
			return fail(&FileError{
				Operation: "create-temp",
				Path:      filepath.Join(stateDir, name),
				Err:       cause,
			}, cleanup)
		}
		if temp.identity.numberOfLinks != 1 {
			closeErr := closePinnedObject(f.api, &temp)
			return fail(&FileError{
				Operation: "link-count",
				Path:      filepath.Join(stateDir, name),
				Err:       ErrUnsafeHardLink,
			}, closeErr)
		}
		return temp, nil
	}
	return fail(&FileError{
		Operation: "create-temp",
		Path:      stateDir,
		Err:       windows.ERROR_FILE_EXISTS,
	}, nil)
}

func randomStateTempName() (string, error) {
	var random [16]byte
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(rand.Reader, random[:]); err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("read state temp entropy: %w", err)
	}
	return ".auto-mas-state-" + hex.EncodeToString(random[:]) + ".tmp", nil
}

func writeAllHandle(
	ctx context.Context,
	handle windows.Handle,
	payload []byte,
	api pathAPI,
) error {
	for len(payload) > 0 {
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		written, err := api.writeFile(handle, payload)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if written <= 0 || written > len(payload) {
			return io.ErrShortWrite
		}
		payload = payload[written:]
	}
	return nil
}

func (f *StateFiles) inspectStateDestination(
	ctx context.Context,
	path string,
) (pinnedObject, bool, error, error) {
	spec := openSpec{
		access:    windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES | windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE | windows.FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
		creation:  windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
		options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
		directory: false,
	}
	destination, err := openRelativeCheckedWith(
		ctx,
		f.pins[1],
		filepath.Base(path),
		spec,
		f.api,
	)
	if errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND) ||
		errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_PATH_NOT_FOUND) {
		return pinnedObject{}, false, nil, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		cause, cleanup := splitOperationCleanup(err)
		return pinnedObject{}, false, cause, cleanup
	}
	if destination.identity.numberOfLinks != 1 {
		closeErr := closePinnedObject(f.api, &destination)
		return pinnedObject{}, false,
			&FileError{Operation: "link-count", Path: path, Err: ErrUnsafeHardLink},
			closeErr
	}
	return destination, true, nil, nil
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 4：实现 RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged**

先拒绝零值、foreign owner、invalid kind 和 `StateEnvironment`。以 snapshot kind 的
固定 Layout 路径打开 leaf，不授予 share-write/delete；不存在返回 false/nil。保持同一
leaf handle，依次比较 volume、file ID、ordinary、link count、长度、SHA-256 和字节；
任一不符包装 `ErrIdentityChanged`。全部一致才
`deleteByHandleWith(leaf.handle, f.api)`，成功先冻结
`MutationApplied=true` 再 close。

```go
func (f *StateFiles) RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged(
	ctx context.Context,
	snapshot StateFileSnapshot,
) (RemoveResult, error) {
	if ctx == nil {
		return RemoveResult{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: context is nil", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return RemoveResult{}, err
	}
	if snapshot.owner == nil || snapshot.owner != f.owner ||
		!snapshot.kind.Valid() || snapshot.kind == StateEnvironment ||
		len(snapshot.payload) == 0 ||
		snapshot.digest != sha256.Sum256(snapshot.payload) {
		return RemoveResult{}, ErrInvalidToken
	}
	path, err := statePath(f.layout, snapshot.kind)
	if err != nil {
		return RemoveResult{}, err
	}

	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if f.closed {
		return RemoveResult{}, ErrClosed
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return RemoveResult{}, err
	}
	spec := openSpec{
		access: windows.FILE_READ_DATA |
			windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES |
			windows.DELETE |
			windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ,
		creation:  windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
		options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
		directory: false,
	}
	leaf, err := openRelativeCheckedWith(
		ctx,
		f.pins[1],
		filepath.Base(path),
		spec,
		f.api,
	)
	if errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND) ||
		errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_PATH_NOT_FOUND) {
		return RemoveResult{}, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return RemoveResult{}, err
	}
	closeWith := func(operationErr error) (RemoveResult, error) {
		return RemoveResult{}, errors.Join(operationErr, closePinnedObject(f.api, &leaf))
	}
	if leaf.identity.volumeSerial != snapshot.volumeSerial ||
		leaf.identity.fileID != snapshot.fileID ||
		leaf.identity.numberOfLinks != 1 ||
		leaf.identity.size != int64(len(snapshot.payload)) {
		return closeWith(ErrIdentityChanged)
	}
	payload, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(
		&handleReader{handle: leaf.handle, api: f.api},
		MaxStateFileBytes+1,
	))
	if err != nil {
		return closeWith(&FileError{Operation: "read", Path: path, Err: err})
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(payload, snapshot.payload) ||
		sha256.Sum256(payload) != snapshot.digest {
		return closeWith(ErrIdentityChanged)
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return closeWith(err)
	}
	if err := deleteByHandleWith(leaf.handle, f.api); err != nil {
		return closeWith(&FileError{Operation: "remove", Path: path, Err: err})
	}
	result := RemoveResult{MutationApplied: true}
	if err := closePinnedObject(f.api, &leaf); err != nil {
		return result, err
	}
	return result, nil
}
```

```go
package filesystem

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"testing"

	"golang.org/x/sys/windows"

	"github.com/AUTO-MAS-Project/AUTO-MAS-Runtime/internal/config"
)

func TestNewDownloadFiles_RejectsInvalidLayoutAndRoot(t *testing.T) {
	if files, err := NewDownloadFiles(nil); files != nil || !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument) {
		t.Fatalf("NewDownloadFiles(nil) = %#v, %v", files, err)
	}
	parent := t.TempDir()
	external := t.TempDir()
	root := filepath.Join(parent, "app")
	if err := os.Symlink(external, root); err != nil {
		t.Skipf("directory symlink unavailable: %v", err)
	}
	layout, err := config.NewLayout(root, parent)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("config.NewLayout() error = %v", err)
	}
	if files, err := NewDownloadFiles(layout); files != nil || err == nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewDownloadFiles(reparse root) = %#v, %v, want rejection", files, err)
	}
}

func TestNewDownloadFiles_ValidatesThenClosesAllHandles(t *testing.T) {
	layout := newDownloadTestLayout(t)
	api := newProductionPathAPI()
	openHandles := 0
	openPath := api.openPath
	openRelative := api.openRelative
	closeHandle := api.closeHandle
	api.openPath = func(path string, spec openSpec) (windows.Handle, error) {
		handle, err := openPath(path, spec)
		if err == nil {
			openHandles++
		}
		return handle, err
	}
	api.openRelative = func(
		parent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		spec openSpec,
	) (windows.Handle, error) {
		handle, err := openRelative(parent, name, spec)
		if err == nil {
			openHandles++
		}
		return handle, err
	}
	api.closeHandle = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		err := closeHandle(handle)
		if err == nil {
			openHandles--
		}
		return err
	}
	files, err := newDownloadFilesWith(layout, downloadFileDependencies{api: api})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("newDownloadFilesWith() error = %v", err)
	}
	if files == nil || openHandles != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("files/open handles = %#v/%d, want non-nil/0", files, openHandles)
	}
}

func TestNewDownloadFiles_DoesNotCreateOrRetainDirectories(t *testing.T) {
	layout := newDownloadTestLayout(t)
	files, err := NewDownloadFiles(layout)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewDownloadFiles() error = %v", err)
	}
	if files == nil {
		t.Fatal("NewDownloadFiles() = nil, want capability")
	}
	for _, path := range []string{
		layout.RuntimeDir(),
		layout.RuntimeCacheDir(),
		layout.DownloadCacheDir(),
	} {
		if _, err := os.Stat(path); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
			t.Fatalf("os.Stat(%q) error = %v, want not-exist", path, err)
		}
	}
	renamed := layout.AppRoot() + "-renamed"
	if err := os.Rename(layout.AppRoot(), renamed); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("app root retained by constructor: %v", err)
	}
}

	layout := newDownloadTestLayout(t)
	api := newProductionPathAPI()
	closeHandle := api.closeHandle
	injected := errors.New("close failed")
	inject := true
	api.closeHandle = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		err := closeHandle(handle)
		if inject {
			inject = false
			return errors.Join(injected, err)
		}
		return err
	}
	files, err := newDownloadFilesWith(layout, downloadFileDependencies{api: api})
	if files != nil || !errors.Is(err, injected) {
		t.Fatalf("newDownloadFilesWith() = %#v, %v, want nil/injected", files, err)
	}
}

func TestDownloadFiles_ConcurrentDifferentNames(t *testing.T) {
	files, _ := newDownloadFixture(t)
	const count = 32
	start := make(chan struct{})
	errs := make(chan error, count)
	var wait sync.WaitGroup
	wait.Add(count)
	for i := 0; i < count; i++ {
		name := fmt.Sprintf("part-%02d.zip", i)
		go func() {
			defer wait.Done()
			<-start
			session, err := files.Begin(t.Context(), name)
			if err == nil {
				_, err = session.Write([]byte(name))
			}
			if err == nil {
				_, err = session.Abort(t.Context())
			}
			errs <- err
		}()
	}
	close(start)
	wait.Wait()
	close(errs)
	for err := range errs {
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("concurrent session error = %v", err)
		}
	}
}

func newDownloadFixture(t *testing.T) (*DownloadFiles, *config.Layout) {
	t.Helper()
	layout := newDownloadTestLayout(t)
	files, err := NewDownloadFiles(layout)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewDownloadFiles() error = %v", err)
	}
	return files, layout
}

func newDownloadTestLayout(t *testing.T) *config.Layout {
	t.Helper()
	root := t.TempDir()
	layout, err := config.NewLayout(root, filepath.Dir(root))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("config.NewLayout() error = %v", err)
	}
	return layout
}
```

Begin 测试核对 final / part 精确等于 Layout 的 `DownloadFile` /
`DownloadPartFile`。final 存在时只有直接普通单链接文件才返回
`ErrDestinationExists`；Reparse、目录或硬链接必须安全错误且不改动。stale part 只有
普通单链接对象才通过同一 handle 删除；另一个 active session 的 part 因不授予
share-delete 返回 occupied，不能被“清理”。

构造测试证明无 ctx 的 NewDownloadFiles 同步规范化并验证 app-root、`RuntimeDir()`、
`RuntimeCacheDir()`、`DownloadCacheDir()` 的 Layout 关系、local volume、Reparse 和
case-sensitivity；它不得创建缺失目录。成功或失败返回前均逆序关闭所有校验 handles，
close 故障用 `errors.Join` 返回且不返回能力。构造成功后可立即 rename 已存在祖先，
handle ledger 必须为零；`DownloadFiles` 只保存 Layout 与 `api`。

Begin 测试从只有 app-root 的夹具开始，每次调用都逐级创建缺失目录，并重新打开、固定
app-root/runtime/cache/downloads 四层，不复用构造时结论或上一次 session 的 handle。
第一条 session Abort 并释放后，把任一祖先替换成 Junction，再次 Begin 必须重新检查并
失败关闭；任一级创建后核验失败只关闭本次 handles，不删除刚创建目录。

context 表对 Begin / Abort 传 nil 和预取消 context，断言零 create/open/disposition。
权限测试逐字段断言四层 directory pin、final inspection、stale part removal、new part
四类 `openSpec`；每次 leaf open 后 parent identity 必须等于本次 session 固定的
downloads parent。

不同 name 用关闭 channel 同时启动 32 个 Begin/Write/Abort，不使用 sleep；每个 session
只能影响自己的 file ID 和路径。

- [ ] **步骤 2：运行红灯**

```powershell
Invoke-ExpectedFailure `
    "Test(NewDownloadFiles|DownloadFiles_(Begin|Concurrent)|DownloadSession_(Write|AbortContext))" `
    "(undefined: NewDownloadFiles|has no field or method Begin|active part was removed)"
```

- [ ] **步骤 3：实现 Begin 与 Write**

`NewDownloadFiles` 构造局部 production dependencies 并调用冻结的
`newDownloadFilesWith`。private constructor 使用 `context.Background()` 仅作为同步
Win32 校验参数，取得四个 Layout 路径并调用
`validateDangerousRootWith`；它只验证当前已存在前缀，不创建 runtime/cache/downloads。
无论成功还是失败，都在返回前调用每条 chain 自带的 `close` 并用 `errors.Join` 保留
校验与 close 错误。只有全部验证和关闭成功才返回
`&DownloadFiles{layout: layout, api: dependencies.api}`；不得保存任何 handle、
不得定义公共 Close 或 `(*DownloadFiles).closeForTest`。

Begin 先检查 ctx，再从 Layout 取得 final / part 与四层目录；随后仅使用 `f.api` 从
app-root 开始逐级创建缺失的 runtime/cache/downloads，并按 directory-pin spec 打开、
验证和固定 app-root/runtime/cache/downloads 四个 `pinnedObject`。四层全部固定后，
以本次 downloads parent 相对检查 final / stale part / create-exclusive part。
任一步失败逆序关闭本次 handles；若 part 已创建则先通过其同一 handle disposition，
但不删除本次新建的普通目录。final 与 part 均只来自 Layout getter。

```go
type downloadState uint8

const (
	downloadOpen downloadState = iota
	downloadPublished
	downloadAborted
)

type DownloadSession struct {
	mu sync.Mutex // 保护 part、pins、state、结果和 closeErr。

	api       pathAPI
	path      string
	partPath  string
	part      pinnedObject
	pins      [4]pinnedObject
	state     downloadState
	published PublishResult
	abort     AbortResult
	closeErr  error
}
```

按设计顺序检查 final、处理 safe stale part、create-exclusive part、核验并保存 identity。
成功返回前把 `f.api` 和本次四层 pins 复制到 session。Write 持 session mutex，只允许
open；每次写前调用 `s.api.identity` 查询同一 part handle 的 volume/file ID/
ordinary/link count，再调用 `s.api.writeFile`。`Path` 返回 final，`PartPath` 返回 part，均为
绝对路径。为保证本任务测试和实际调用都不会泄漏 `.part`，本任务同时实现 open 状态的
Abort：通过 `deleteByHandleWith(s.part.handle, s.api)`、先冻结 aborted /
Removed=true、再用 `s.api.closeHandle` 关闭 part 与四层 pins；published 拒绝、
aborted 返回缓存结果。`TestDownloadSession_WriteAndCloseUseStoredAPI` 覆盖 write /
close / disposition 三类 field。任务 8 用故障注入补齐重复 Abort、
post-disposition close error 和 Publish 交叉状态，不重新定义方法。

同包测试在 `download_files_windows_test.go` 实现前文精确签名的
`(*DownloadSession).closeForTest() error`，只供 `t.Cleanup` 释放故障用例尚持有的
handle；它不是 DownloadFiles 生命周期接口。

本步骤完整实现如下。构造校验只固定并关闭每个目标的最近已存在前缀；Begin 才创建目录：

```go
func NewDownloadFiles(layout *config.Layout) (*DownloadFiles, error) {
	return newDownloadFilesWith(layout, downloadFileDependencies{
		api: newProductionPathAPI(),
	})
}

func newDownloadFilesWith(
	layout *config.Layout,
	dependencies downloadFileDependencies,
) (*DownloadFiles, error) {
	if layout == nil || !dependencies.api.valid() {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid download-file dependencies", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	ctx := context.Background()
	targets := []string{
		layout.AppRoot(),
		layout.RuntimeDir(),
		layout.RuntimeCacheDir(),
		layout.DownloadCacheDir(),
	}
	for _, target := range targets {
		if err := validateExistingLayoutPathWith(ctx, layout, target, dependencies.api); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return &DownloadFiles{layout: layout, api: dependencies.api}, nil
}

func validateExistingLayoutPathWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	target string,
	api pathAPI,
) error {
	appRoot, err := canonicalizeWith(layout.AppRoot(), api)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	canonical, err := canonicalizeWith(target, api)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !appRoot.Equal(canonical) && !appRoot.Contains(canonical) {
		return outsidePathError("validate-download-root", canonical.String(), ErrIdentityChanged)
	}
	existing := canonical.String()
	for {
		_, err := api.attributes(nativeWindowsPath(existing))
		if err == nil {
			break
		}
		if !isWindowsNotFound(err) {
			return &FileError{Operation: "attributes", Path: existing, Err: err}
		}
		parent := filepath.Dir(existing)
		if parent == existing {
			return &FileError{Operation: "attributes", Path: existing, Err: err}
		}
		existing = parent
	}
	existingCanonical, err := canonicalizeWith(existing, api)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	rootParent, err := canonicalizeWith(filepath.Dir(layout.AppRoot()), api)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	chain, err := openPinnedChainWith(
		ctx,
		rootParent,
		existingCanonical,
		directoryPinSpec(),
		api,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return chain.close()
}

func (f *DownloadFiles) Begin(
	ctx context.Context,
	name string,
) (*DownloadSession, error) {
	if ctx == nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: context is nil", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	finalPath, err := f.layout.DownloadFile(name)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: download final path: %v", ErrInvalidArgument, err)
	}
	partPath, err := f.layout.DownloadPartFile(name)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: download part path: %v", ErrInvalidArgument, err)
	}
	pins, err := f.pinDownloadRoots(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	fail := func(operationErr error) (*DownloadSession, error) {
		return nil, errors.Join(operationErr, closePinnedObjects(f.api, pins[:]))
	}

	finalSpec := openSpec{
		access:    windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES | windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE | windows.FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
		creation:  windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
		options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
		directory: false,
	}
	final, err := openRelativeCheckedWith(
		ctx,
		pins[3],
		filepath.Base(finalPath),
		finalSpec,
		f.api,
	)
	if err == nil {
		closeErr := closePinnedObject(f.api, &final)
		if final.identity.numberOfLinks != 1 {
			return fail(errors.Join(ErrUnsafeHardLink, closeErr))
		}
		return fail(errors.Join(ErrDestinationExists, closeErr))
	}
	if !isWindowsNotFound(err) {
		return fail(err)
	}

	staleSpec := openSpec{
		access:    windows.DELETE | windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES | windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
		creation:  windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
		options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
		directory: false,
	}
	stale, err := openRelativeCheckedWith(
		ctx,
		pins[3],
		filepath.Base(partPath),
		staleSpec,
		f.api,
	)
	if err == nil {
		if stale.identity.numberOfLinks != 1 {
			return fail(errors.Join(
				ErrUnsafeHardLink,
				closePinnedObject(f.api, &stale),
			))
		}
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return fail(errors.Join(err, closePinnedObject(f.api, &stale)))
		}
		if err := deleteByHandleWith(stale.handle, f.api); err != nil {
			return fail(errors.Join(err, closePinnedObject(f.api, &stale)))
		}
		if err := closePinnedObject(f.api, &stale); err != nil {
			return fail(err)
		}
	} else if !isWindowsNotFound(err) {
		return fail(err)
	}

	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return fail(err)
	}
	partSpec := openSpec{
		access: windows.FILE_WRITE_DATA |
			windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES |
			windows.DELETE |
			windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ,
		creation:  windows.CREATE_NEW,
		options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
		directory: false,
	}
	part, err := openRelativeCheckedWith(
		context.WithoutCancel(ctx),
		pins[3],
		filepath.Base(partPath),
		partSpec,
		f.api,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return fail(err)
	}
	if part.identity.numberOfLinks != 1 {
		cleanupErr := errors.Join(
			deleteByHandleWith(part.handle, f.api),
			closePinnedObject(f.api, &part),
		)
		return fail(errors.Join(ErrUnsafeHardLink, cleanupErr))
	}
	return &DownloadSession{
		api:      f.api,
		path:     finalPath,
		partPath: partPath,
		part:     part,
		pins:     pins,
		state:    downloadOpen,
	}, nil
}

func (f *DownloadFiles) pinDownloadRoots(
	ctx context.Context,
) ([4]pinnedObject, error) {
	var result [4]pinnedObject
	appPath, err := canonicalizeWith(f.layout.AppRoot(), f.api)
	if err != nil {
		return result, err
	}
	rootParent, err := canonicalizeWith(filepath.Dir(f.layout.AppRoot()), f.api)
	if err != nil {
		return result, err
	}
	appChain, err := openPinnedChainWith(
		ctx,
		rootParent,
		appPath,
		directoryPinSpec(),
		f.api,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return result, err
	}
	app, err := detachLeaf(appChain)
	if err != nil {
		return result, err
	}
	pins := make([]pinnedObject, 0, 4)
	pins = append(pins, app)
	parent := app
	targets := []string{
		f.layout.RuntimeDir(),
		f.layout.RuntimeCacheDir(),
		f.layout.DownloadCacheDir(),
	}
	for _, target := range targets {
		canonical, err := canonicalizeWith(target, f.api)
		if err != nil {
			return result, errors.Join(err, closePinnedObjects(f.api, pins))
		}
		child, err := openOrCreatePinnedDirectory(ctx, parent, canonical, f.api)
		if err != nil {
			return result, errors.Join(err, closePinnedObjects(f.api, pins))
		}
		pins = append(pins, child)
		parent = child
	}
	copy(result[:], pins)
	return result, nil
}

func (s *DownloadSession) Write(p []byte) (int, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.state != downloadOpen {
		return 0, ErrClosed
	}
	identity, err := s.api.identity(s.part.handle)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, &FileError{Operation: "identify", Path: s.partPath, Err: err}
	}
	if identity.volumeSerial != s.part.identity.volumeSerial ||
		identity.fileID != s.part.identity.fileID {
		return 0, ErrIdentityChanged
	}
	if identity.numberOfLinks != 1 ||
		identity.attributes&(windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY|windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT) != 0 {
		return 0, ErrUnsafeHardLink
	}
	n, err := s.api.writeFile(s.part.handle, p)
	if err != nil {
		return n, &FileError{Operation: "write", Path: s.partPath, Err: err}
	}
	return n, nil
}

func (s *DownloadSession) Path() string {
	return s.path
}

func (s *DownloadSession) PartPath() string {
	return s.partPath
}

func (s *DownloadSession) Abort(ctx context.Context) (AbortResult, error) {
	if ctx == nil {
		return AbortResult{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: context is nil", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return AbortResult{}, err
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	switch s.state {
	case downloadAborted:
		return s.abort, s.closeErr
	case downloadPublished:
		return AbortResult{}, ErrClosed
	case downloadOpen:
	default:
		return AbortResult{}, ErrClosed
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return AbortResult{}, err
	}
	if err := deleteByHandleWith(s.part.handle, s.api); err != nil {
		return AbortResult{}, &FileError{Operation: "abort", Path: s.partPath, Err: err}
	}
	s.state = downloadAborted
	s.abort = AbortResult{Removed: true}
	s.closeErr = errors.Join(
		closePinnedObject(s.api, &s.part),
		closePinnedObjects(s.api, s.pins[:]),
	)
	return s.abort, s.closeErr
}
```

`validateExistingLayoutPathWith` 先用 `CanonicalPath.Equal` / `Contains` 完成
app-root 边界授权，再只向父级寻找已存在前缀；它不构造或暴露新的未核验
`CanonicalPath`。

- [ ] **步骤 1：编写状态机和故障语义失败测试**

把以下完整函数追加到 `download_files_windows_test.go`：

```go
func TestDownloadSession_PublishNoReplaceFlushesAndKeepsFileID(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	if _, err := session.Write([]byte("payload")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Write() error = %v", err)
	}
	originalID := session.part.identity.fileID
	result, err := session.PublishNoReplace(t.Context())
	if err != nil || !result.Published {
		t.Fatalf("PublishNoReplace() = %#v, %v", result, err)
	}
	got, err := os.ReadFile(session.Path())
	if err != nil || string(got) != "payload" {
		t.Fatalf("final payload = %q, error = %v", got, err)
	}
	api := newProductionPathAPI()
	handle, err := api.openPath(nativeWindowsPath(session.Path()), openSpec{
		access:    windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES | windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
		share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE | windows.FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
		creation:  windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
		options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
		directory: false,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("open final error = %v", err)
	}
	identity, identityErr := api.identity(handle)
	closeErr := api.closeHandle(handle)
	if identityErr != nil || closeErr != nil {
		t.Fatalf("final identity/close errors = %v/%v", identityErr, closeErr)
	}
	if identity.fileID != originalID {
		t.Fatalf("final file ID = %x, want %x", identity.fileID, originalID)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_PublishNeverReplacesCompetitor(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	if _, err := session.Write([]byte("session")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Write() error = %v", err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(session.Path(), []byte("competitor"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile(competitor) error = %v", err)
	}
	result, err := session.PublishNoReplace(t.Context())
	if result.Published || !errors.Is(err, ErrDestinationExists) {
		t.Fatalf("PublishNoReplace() = %#v, %v, want occupied", result, err)
	}
	got, _ := os.ReadFile(session.Path())
	if string(got) != "competitor" {
		t.Fatalf("competitor = %q, want unchanged", got)
	}
	if _, err := session.Abort(t.Context()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Abort() error = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_PublishFailureRemainsAbortable(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	injected := errors.New("rename failed")
	session.api.rename = func(
		windows.Handle,
		windows.Handle,
		string,
		bool,
	) error {
		return injected
	}
	result, err := session.PublishNoReplace(t.Context())
	if result.Published || !errors.Is(err, injected) {
		t.Fatalf("PublishNoReplace() = %#v, %v", result, err)
	}
	abort, err := session.Abort(t.Context())
	if err != nil || !abort.Removed {
		t.Fatalf("Abort() = %#v, %v, want removed", abort, err)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_PublishReportsPublishedBeforeCloseError(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	injected := errors.New("close failed")
	closeHandle := session.api.closeHandle
	rename := session.api.rename
	renamed := false
	session.api.rename = func(
		source windows.Handle,
		parent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		replace bool,
	) error {
		err := rename(source, parent, name, replace)
		renamed = err == nil
		return err
	}
	session.api.closeHandle = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		err := closeHandle(handle)
		if renamed {
			renamed = false
			return errors.Join(injected, err)
		}
		return err
	}
	result, err := session.PublishNoReplace(t.Context())
	if !result.Published || !errors.Is(err, injected) {
		t.Fatalf("PublishNoReplace() = %#v, %v, want published/injected", result, err)
	}
	if _, abortErr := session.Abort(t.Context()); !errors.Is(abortErr, ErrClosed) {
		t.Fatalf("Abort() error = %v, want ErrClosed", abortErr)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_AbortDeletesBySameHandle(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	originalID := session.part.identity.fileID
	seenID := [16]byte{}
	identity := session.api.identity
	setDisposition := session.api.setDisposition
	session.api.setDisposition = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		got, err := identity(handle)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		seenID = got.fileID
		return setDisposition(handle)
	}
	result, err := session.Abort(t.Context())
	if err != nil || !result.Removed {
		t.Fatalf("Abort() = %#v, %v", result, err)
	}
	if seenID != originalID {
		t.Fatalf("disposed file ID = %x, want %x", seenID, originalID)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_AbortIsIdempotentAfterCloseError(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	injected := errors.New("close failed")
	closeHandle := session.api.closeHandle
	dispositionCalls := 0
	setDisposition := session.api.setDisposition
	session.api.setDisposition = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		dispositionCalls++
		return setDisposition(handle)
	}
	session.api.closeHandle = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		err := closeHandle(handle)
		return errors.Join(injected, err)
	}
	first, firstErr := session.Abort(t.Context())
	second, secondErr := session.Abort(t.Context())
	if !first.Removed || second != first ||
		!errors.Is(firstErr, injected) || !errors.Is(secondErr, injected) {
		t.Fatalf("Abort results = %#v/%#v, errors = %v/%v", first, second, firstErr, secondErr)
	}
	if dispositionCalls != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("disposition calls = %d, want 1", dispositionCalls)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_AbortRejectsPublishedSession(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	if result, err := session.PublishNoReplace(t.Context()); err != nil || !result.Published {
		t.Fatalf("PublishNoReplace() = %#v, %v", result, err)
	}
	if result, err := session.Abort(t.Context()); result.Removed || !errors.Is(err, ErrClosed) {
		t.Fatalf("Abort() = %#v, %v, want state rejection", result, err)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_StateTransitionsAreLinearized(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	start := make(chan struct{})
	type outcome struct {
		published bool
		removed   bool
		err       error
	}
	outcomes := make(chan outcome, 2)
	go func() {
		<-start
		result, err := session.PublishNoReplace(t.Context())
		outcomes <- outcome{published: result.Published, err: err}
	}()
	go func() {
		<-start
		result, err := session.Abort(t.Context())
		outcomes <- outcome{removed: result.Removed, err: err}
	}()
	close(start)
	first := <-outcomes
	second := <-outcomes
	successes := 0
	for _, result := range []outcome{first, second} {
		if result.published || result.removed {
			successes++
		}
	}
	if successes != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("transition outcomes = %#v/%#v, want exactly one success", first, second)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_PublishAndAbortContextsRejectedBeforeIO(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = session.closeForTest() })
	flushCalls := 0
	dispositionCalls := 0
	session.api.flushFile = func(windows.Handle) error {
		flushCalls++
		return nil
	}
	session.api.setDisposition = func(windows.Handle) error {
		dispositionCalls++
		return nil
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	cancel()
	if _, err := session.PublishNoReplace(ctx); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
		t.Fatalf("PublishNoReplace() error = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := session.Abort(nil); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument) {
		t.Fatalf("Abort(nil) error = %v", err)
	}
	if flushCalls != 0 || dispositionCalls != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("I/O calls = flush %d/disposition %d, want 0/0", flushCalls, dispositionCalls)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_PublishCancellationAfterRenameKeepsPublished(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	rename := session.api.rename
	session.api.rename = func(
		source windows.Handle,
		parent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		replace bool,
	) error {
		err := rename(source, parent, name, replace)
		if err == nil {
			cancel()
		}
		return err
	}
	result, err := session.PublishNoReplace(ctx)
	if err != nil || !result.Published {
		t.Fatalf("PublishNoReplace() = %#v, %v, want published/nil", result, err)
	}
}

func TestDownloadSession_AbortCancellationAfterDispositionKeepsRemoved(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	setDisposition := session.api.setDisposition
	session.api.setDisposition = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
		err := setDisposition(handle)
		if err == nil {
			cancel()
		}
		return err
	}
	result, err := session.Abort(ctx)
	if err != nil || !result.Removed {
		t.Fatalf("Abort() = %#v, %v, want removed/nil", result, err)
	}
}

func TestWindows_DownloadPublishesValidatedHandleWithoutReplacement(t *testing.T) {
	session := newDownloadSessionForTest(t)
	if _, err := session.Write([]byte("validated")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Write() error = %v", err)
	}
	if result, err := session.PublishNoReplace(t.Context()); err != nil || !result.Published {
		t.Fatalf("PublishNoReplace() = %#v, %v", result, err)
	}
	got, err := os.ReadFile(session.Path())
	if err != nil || string(got) != "validated" {
		t.Fatalf("published file = %q, error = %v", got, err)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(session.PartPath()); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		t.Fatalf("part path still exists: %v", err)
	}
}

func newDownloadSessionForTest(t *testing.T) *DownloadSession {
	t.Helper()
	files, _ := newDownloadFixture(t)
	session, err := files.Begin(t.Context(), "uv.zip")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Begin() error = %v", err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = session.closeForTest() })
	return session
}
```

Publish 前 barrier 在外部校验完成后尝试替换 `.part` 路径；session 必须仍发布自己持有的
file ID，不能打开路径上的竞争者。另一个 barrier 在 rename 前创建 final；no-replace
rename 返回 occupied，竞争者逐字节不变，session 保持 open 并可 Abort。

rename 成功、首个 close 失败时固定断言 `Published=true`、final 保持发布内容、
Abort 返回状态错误且不 disposition final。Abort disposition 成功、close 失败时固定
`Removed=true` 并缓存 aborted result，重复 Abort 不再调用 disposition。

context 表对 PublishNoReplace / Abort 传 nil 和预取消 context，断言零 flush/rename/
disposition。提交点 barrier 分别在 rename 与 disposition 成功后取消；结果必须保持
`Published=true` / `Removed=true`，取消不能触发 Abort final 或重复 disposition。
Windows 对抗测试在校验后替换 part 路径、rename 前创建 final 竞争者，证明发布对象仍是
session file ID，竞争者永不覆盖。

```powershell
Invoke-ExpectedFailure `
    "Test(DownloadSession_(Publish|Abort|State)|Windows_Download)" `
    "(has no field or method PublishNoReplace|got Published false|final competitor was overwritten)"
```

- [ ] **步骤 3：实现 PublishNoReplace 并补齐 Abort 关闭故障缓存**

Publish 持 session mutex，仅 open 可进入；检查 ctx 并用 `s.api.identity` 重验同一
part，通过 `s.api.flushFile` 刷盘；`finalName := filepath.Base(s.path)`，再调用
`renameByHandleWith(s.part.handle, s.pins[3].handle, finalName, false, s.api)`，以本次
session 固定的 downloads parent 为 RootDirectory、flags 0 发布。rename 前失败保持
open、`Published=false`；rename 成功先置 `downloadPublished` 并缓存
`PublishResult{Published: true}`，再只用 `s.api.closeHandle` 关闭 part 与全部 pins。

保留任务 7 的 Abort disposition 和状态转换；本任务增加第一次 close error 缓存：
published 返回状态 `FileError`；aborted 返回缓存 result 和同一缓存 close error；
open disposition 成功后置 `downloadAborted`、`Removed=true`，再关闭全部 handles。
session 不实现公共 Close；故障用例通过同包 `closeForTest` 做 `t.Cleanup`，生产路径不
调用该 helper。

```go
func (s *DownloadSession) PublishNoReplace(
	ctx context.Context,
) (PublishResult, error) {
	if ctx == nil {
		return PublishResult{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: context is nil", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return PublishResult{}, err
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	switch s.state {
	case downloadPublished:
		return s.published, s.closeErr
	case downloadAborted:
		return PublishResult{}, ErrClosed
	case downloadOpen:
	default:
		return PublishResult{}, ErrClosed
	}
	identity, err := s.api.identity(s.part.handle)
	if err != nil {
		return PublishResult{}, &FileError{
			Operation: "identify",
			Path:      s.partPath,
			Err:       err,
		}
	}
	if identity.volumeSerial != s.part.identity.volumeSerial ||
		identity.fileID != s.part.identity.fileID {
		return PublishResult{}, ErrIdentityChanged
	}
	if identity.numberOfLinks != 1 ||
		identity.attributes&(windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY|windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT) != 0 {
		return PublishResult{}, ErrUnsafeHardLink
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return PublishResult{}, err
	}
	if err := s.api.flushFile(s.part.handle); err != nil {
		return PublishResult{}, &FileError{
			Operation: "flush",
			Path:      s.partPath,
			Err:       err,
		}
	}
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return PublishResult{}, err
	}
	if err := renameByHandleWith(
		s.part.handle,
		s.pins[3].handle,
		filepath.Base(s.path),
		false,
		s.api,
	); err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS) ||
			errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_FILE_EXISTS) {
			return PublishResult{}, errors.Join(ErrDestinationExists, err)
		}
		return PublishResult{}, &FileError{
			Operation: "publish",
			Path:      s.path,
			Err:       err,
		}
	}
	s.state = downloadPublished
	s.published = PublishResult{Published: true}
	s.closeErr = errors.Join(
		closePinnedObject(s.api, &s.part),
		closePinnedObjects(s.api, s.pins[:]),
	)
	return s.published, s.closeErr
}
```

rename 返回成功是唯一发布提交点；其后不再检查 ctx，也不进入 Abort。rename 前任何
错误保持 `downloadOpen` 和全部 handle，调用方仍能显式 Abort。

以下是 `delete_test.go` 在本任务新增部分的完整内容；已有 Task 1 枚举测试保留在同文件
前部，import 合并后不得重复：

```go
package filesystem

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"golang.org/x/sys/windows"

	"github.com/AUTO-MAS-Project/AUTO-MAS-Runtime/internal/config"
	"github.com/AUTO-MAS-Project/AUTO-MAS-Runtime/internal/protocol"
)

type recordingDeleteAuditor struct {
	records []DeleteAuditRecord
	err     error
}

func (a *recordingDeleteAuditor) RecordDeletion(
	_ context.Context,
	record DeleteAuditRecord,
) error {
	a.records = append(a.records, record)
	return a.err
}

func TestNew_RejectsInvalidLayoutAuditorAndOptions(t *testing.T) {
	layout := newDeleteTestLayout(t)
	auditor := &recordingDeleteAuditor{}
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		ctx     context.Context
		layout  *config.Layout
		auditor Auditor
		options []Option
	}{
		{name: "nil context", ctx: nil, layout: layout, auditor: auditor},
		{name: "nil layout", ctx: t.Context(), auditor: auditor},
		{name: "nil auditor", ctx: t.Context(), layout: layout},
		{name: "nil option", ctx: t.Context(), layout: layout, auditor: auditor, options: []Option{nil}},
		{
			name:    "nil wait",
			ctx:     t.Context(),
			layout:  layout,
			auditor: auditor,
			options: []Option{WithWait(nil)},
		},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			operator, err := New(test.ctx, test.layout, test.auditor, test.options...)
			if operator != nil {
				t.Fatalf("New() operator = %#v, want nil", operator)
			}
			if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument) {
				t.Fatalf("New() error = %v, want ErrInvalidArgument", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestNew_RejectsDuplicateOrUnboundedRenameOptions(t *testing.T) {
	layout := newDeleteTestLayout(t)
	auditor := &recordingDeleteAuditor{}
	wait := func(context.Context, time.Duration) error { return nil }
	seventeen := make([]time.Duration, 17)
	for i := range seventeen {
		seventeen[i] = time.Millisecond
	}
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		options []Option
	}{
		{name: "duplicate wait", options: []Option{WithWait(wait), WithWait(wait)}},
		{
			name: "duplicate delays",
			options: []Option{
				WithRenameDelays(time.Millisecond),
				WithRenameDelays(time.Millisecond),
			},
		},
		{name: "empty delays", options: []Option{WithRenameDelays()}},
		{name: "zero delay", options: []Option{WithRenameDelays(0)}},
		{name: "negative delay", options: []Option{WithRenameDelays(-time.Millisecond)}},
		{name: "seventeen delays", options: []Option{WithRenameDelays(seventeen...)}},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			operator, err := New(t.Context(), layout, auditor, test.options...)
			if operator != nil {
				t.Fatalf("New() operator = %#v, want nil", operator)
			}
			if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument) {
				t.Fatalf("New() error = %v, want ErrInvalidArgument", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestNew_ContextRejectedBeforeIO(t *testing.T) {
	layout := newDeleteTestLayout(t)
	auditor := &recordingDeleteAuditor{}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	cancel()
	operator, err := New(ctx, layout, auditor)
	if operator != nil {
		t.Fatalf("New() operator = %#v, want nil", operator)
	}

		t.Fatalf("New() error = %v, want context.Canceled", err)
	}

}

func TestNew_MatchesDirectoryPinMatrixAndParentIdentity(t *testing.T) {
	layout := newDeleteTestLayout(t)
	auditor := &recordingDeleteAuditor{}
	api := newProductionPathAPI()
	openPath := api.openPath
	var specs []openSpec
	api.openPath = func(path string, spec openSpec) (windows.Handle, error) {
		specs = append(specs, spec)
		return openPath(path, spec)
	}
	_, err := newWithDependencies(
		t.Context(),
		layout,
		auditor,
		options{wait: defaultWait, delays: defaultRenameDelays()},
		operatorDependencies{
			api: api,
			finishedContext: func(
				ctx context.Context,
			) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc) {
				return context.WithCancel(context.WithoutCancel(ctx))
			},
		},
	)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("newWithDependencies() error = %v", err)
	}
	if len(specs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("directory pin specs = 0, want at least 1")
	}
	found := false
	for _, spec := range specs {
		if spec == directoryPinSpec() {
			found = true
			break
		}
	}
	if !found {
		t.Fatalf("open specs = %#v, want directory pin spec", specs)
	}
}

func TestAuthorizeDeleteRequest_AcceptsOnlyExactLayoutIdentity(t *testing.T) {
	fixture := newDeleteFixture(t)
	type requestCase struct {
		name          string
		kind          DeleteKind
		target        string
		version       string
		dynamicTarget func(operationID string) string
	}
	repoUpdate := func(operationID string) string {
		path, err := fixture.layout.RepoUpdateDir(operationID)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("RepoUpdateDir() error = %v", err)
		}
		return path
	}
	repoRetired := func(operationID string) string {
		path, err := fixture.layout.RepoPreviousDir(operationID)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("RepoPreviousDir() error = %v", err)
		}
		return path
	}
	uvStaging := func(operationID string) string {
		path, err := fixture.layout.UVStagingDir("0.9.0", operationID)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("UVStagingDir() error = %v", err)
		}
		return path
	}
	tests := []requestCase{
		{name: "uv cache", kind: DeleteUVCache, target: fixture.layout.UVCacheDir()},
		{name: "venv", kind: DeleteManagedVenv, target: fixture.layout.VenvDir()},
		{name: "python", kind: DeleteManagedPython, target: fixture.layout.PythonDir()},
		{
			name:          "repo update",
			kind:          DeleteRepositoryUpdate,
			dynamicTarget: repoUpdate,
		},
		{
			name:          "repo retired",
			kind:          DeleteRepositoryRetired,
			dynamicTarget: repoRetired,
		},
		{
			name:   "download temporary",
			kind:   DeleteDownloadTemporary,
			target: fixture.layout.DownloadCacheDir(),
		},
		{
			name:          "uv staging",
			kind:          DeleteUVStaging,
			version:       "0.9.0",
			dynamicTarget: uvStaging,
		},
		{
			name:   "python cache",
			kind:   DeletePythonCache,
			target: filepath.Join(fixture.layout.RepoDir(), "pkg", "__pycache__"),
		},
		{name: "build cache", kind: DeleteBuildCache, target: fixture.layout.BuildCacheDir()},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			target := test.target
			if test.dynamicTarget != nil {
				target = test.dynamicTarget("operation-a")
			}
			request := DeleteRequest{
				Kind:        test.kind,
				Target:      target,
				OperationID: "operation-a",
				Version:     test.version,
				Reason:      "test cleanup",
			}
			authorized, err := fixture.operator.authorizeDeleteRequest(t.Context(), request)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("authorizeDeleteRequest() error = %v", err)
			}
			if closeErr := authorized.chain.close(); closeErr != nil {
				t.Fatalf("authorized chain close error = %v", closeErr)
			}

			if test.dynamicTarget == nil {
				request.OperationID = "another-safe-audit-id"
				authorized, err = fixture.operator.authorizeDeleteRequest(t.Context(), request)
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatalf("fixed identity with alternate OperationID error = %v", err)
				}
				if closeErr := authorized.chain.close(); closeErr != nil {
					t.Fatalf("alternate chain close error = %v", closeErr)
				}
				return
			}
			request.OperationID = "operation-b"
			_, err = fixture.operator.authorizeDeleteRequest(t.Context(), request)
			assertPathOutside(t, err)
		})
	}
}

func TestAuthorizeDeleteRequest_RejectsInvalidFieldsBeforeIO(t *testing.T) {
	fixture := newDeleteFixture(t)
	api := fixture.operator.api
	attributes := api.attributes
	ioCalls := 0
	api.attributes = func(path string) (uint32, error) {
		ioCalls++
		return attributes(path)
	}
	fixture.operator.api = api

	valid := DeleteRequest{
		Kind:        DeleteUVCache,
		Target:      fixture.layout.UVCacheDir(),
		OperationID: "operation-a",
		Reason:      "cleanup",
	}
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		change func(*DeleteRequest)
	}{
		{name: "unknown kind", change: func(r *DeleteRequest) { r.Kind = "unknown" }},
		{name: "empty target", change: func(r *DeleteRequest) { r.Target = "" }},
		{name: "empty operation", change: func(r *DeleteRequest) { r.OperationID = "" }},
		{name: "operation nul", change: func(r *DeleteRequest) { r.OperationID = "a\x00b" }},
		{name: "operation cr", change: func(r *DeleteRequest) { r.OperationID = "a\rb" }},
		{name: "operation lf", change: func(r *DeleteRequest) { r.OperationID = "a\nb" }},
		{name: "empty reason", change: func(r *DeleteRequest) { r.Reason = "" }},
		{name: "reason lf", change: func(r *DeleteRequest) { r.Reason = "a\nb" }},
		{name: "non-staging version", change: func(r *DeleteRequest) { r.Version = "0.9.0" }},
		{
			name: "staging empty version",
			change: func(r *DeleteRequest) {
				r.Kind = DeleteUVStaging
				r.Version = ""
			},
		},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			ioCalls = 0
			request := valid
			test.change(&request)
			_, err := fixture.operator.authorizeDeleteRequest(t.Context(), request)
			if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument) {
				t.Fatalf("authorizeDeleteRequest() error = %v, want ErrInvalidArgument", err)
			}
			if ioCalls != 0 {
				t.Fatalf("I/O calls = %d, want 0", ioCalls)
			}
			if len(fixture.auditor.records) != 0 {
				t.Fatalf("audit calls = %d, want 0", len(fixture.auditor.records))
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestRecordDeleteStarted_FailureReturnsNonAppliedAuditError(t *testing.T) {
	fixture := newDeleteFixture(t)
	injected := errors.New("audit unavailable")
	fixture.auditor.err = injected
	target := mustCanonicalize(t, fixture.layout.BuildCacheDir())
	err := fixture.operator.recordDeleteStarted(t.Context(), authorizedDelete{
		request: DeleteRequest{
			Kind:        DeleteBuildCache,
			Target:      fixture.layout.BuildCacheDir(),
			OperationID: "operation-a",
			Reason:      "cleanup",
		},
		target: target,
	})
	var auditErr *AuditError
	if !errors.As(err, &auditErr) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want *AuditError", err)
	}
	if auditErr.Phase != DeleteAuditStarted || auditErr.MutationApplied {
		t.Fatalf("AuditError = %#v, want started/non-applied", auditErr)
	}
	if !errors.Is(err, injected) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want injected cause", err)
	}
}

type deleteFixture struct {
	layout   *config.Layout
	auditor  *recordingDeleteAuditor
	operator *Operator
}

func newDeleteFixture(t *testing.T) deleteFixture {
	t.Helper()
	layout := newDeleteTestLayout(t)
	if err := os.MkdirAll(layout.RepoDir(), 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.MkdirAll(repo) error = %v", err)
	}
	auditor := &recordingDeleteAuditor{}
	operator, err := New(t.Context(), layout, auditor)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("New() error = %v", err)
	}
	return deleteFixture{layout: layout, auditor: auditor, operator: operator}
}

func newDeleteTestLayout(t *testing.T) *config.Layout {
	t.Helper()
	root := t.TempDir()
	layout, err := config.NewLayout(root, filepath.Dir(root))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("config.NewLayout() error = %v", err)
	}
	return layout
}

func assertPathOutside(t *testing.T, err error) {
	t.Helper()
	var filesystemErr *Error
	if !errors.As(err, &filesystemErr) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want *Error", err)
	}
	if got := filesystemErr.Code(); got != protocol.CodePathOutsideManagedRoot {
		t.Fatalf("Code() = %q, want %q", got, protocol.CodePathOutsideManagedRoot)
	}
}
```

Option 表固定覆盖 nil Option、nil Wait、重复 Wait、重复 delays、空 delays、0、负数、
17 个 delays；均满足 `errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument)` 且 handle/goroutine 计数为 0。

DeleteKind 授权表逐项使用以下唯一字段矩阵：

| Kind | 精确合法目标 | `OperationID` 是否传给 Layout | `Version` |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DeleteUVCache` | `UVCacheDir()` | 否，仅审计 | 必须为空 |
| `DeleteManagedVenv` | `VenvDir()` | 否，仅审计 | 必须为空 |
| `DeleteManagedPython` | `PythonDir()` | 否，仅审计 | 必须为空 |
| `DeleteRepositoryUpdate` | `RepoUpdateDir(operationID)` | 是 | 必须为空 |
| `DeleteRepositoryRetired` | `RepoPreviousDir(operationID)` | 是 | 必须为空 |
| `DeleteDownloadTemporary` | `DownloadCacheDir()` | 否，仅审计 | 必须为空 |
| `DeleteUVStaging` | `UVStagingDir(version, operationID)` | 是 | 安全非空且参与身份 |
| `DeletePythonCache` | `RepoDir()` 下基名严格为 `__pycache__` 的后代 | 否，仅审计 | 必须为空 |
| `DeleteBuildCache` | `BuildCacheDir()` | 否，仅审计 | 必须为空 |

所有九行都先验证 `OperationID` 为非空、无 NUL/CR/LF 的单行安全值，并把原值写入
started/finished 审计。固定目录 kind 与 `DeletePythonCache` 分别以 `"audit-a"` 和
`"audit-b"` 执行同一合法请求，两次都必须授权成功且 expected-target getter 调用参数
完全不含 operation ID。repository update、repository retired 和 UV staging
把 `"operation-a"` 改成另一个合法 `"operation-b"` 后，原 target 与 Layout getter
结果不再相同，才返回 `protocol.CodePathOutsideManagedRoot`。

字段拒绝表必须在任何 Canonicalize/open/Auditor 调用前完成：

| 输入 | 预期错误 |
| --- | --- |
| unknown `Kind`、空 `Target`、空/含 NUL/CR/LF 的 `OperationID` 或 `Reason` | `ErrInvalidArgument` |
| UV staging 的空 `Version` | `ErrInvalidArgument` |
| 任一非 staging kind 的任意非空 `Version`，包括合法版本字面量 | `ErrInvalidArgument` |
| 字段合法但 kind/target 或动态 getter 身份不匹配 | `Error` + `CodePathOutsideManagedRoot` |

测试分别断言上述 error chain 与 Win32/Auditor 计数为 0，不得把字段错误错误映射为路径
越界。特别地，非 staging 的 `Version` 必须在任何 I/O 前拒绝，不能被静默忽略。

New 的 context 表传 nil 与预取消 context，断言零 Canonicalize/open/Auditor 调用。
权限测试逐字段断言 Operator 所有目录固定均使用矩阵的 directory pin spec，parent
identity 不匹配时构造失败并逆序关闭。started 私有门测试只验证审计失败包装
`AuditError{Phase: DeleteAuditStarted, MutationApplied: false}`；不在本任务定义或调用
公开 RemoveTree。
